# Targum Jonathan on II Samuel

תרגום יונתן על שמואל ב

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel)

תרגום מרכזי על ספרי הנביאים שנקרא בציבור בבית הכנסת בתקופת התלמוד, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות של יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the books of the Prophets, read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Targum Jonathan/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Mikraot Gedolot (Segments: 695, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): The Targum of Samuel; trans. E. van Staalduine-Sulman. Leiden: Brill, 2002 (Segments: 695)


## Chapter 1

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:1)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר דָמִית שָׁאוּל וְדָוִד תַּב מִלְמִמְחֵי יַת דְבֵית עֲמָלֵק וִיתֵיב דָוִד בְּצִקְלָג יוֹמִין תְּרֵין:


After Saul died and David had returned from striking those of the House of Amalek, David remained two days in Ziklag.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:2)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וְהָא גַבְרָא אָתָא מִמַשְׁרִיתָא מִלְוַת שָׁאוּל וּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי מְבַזְעִין וְעַפְרָא רְמֵי בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וַהֲוָה בְּמֵיתוֹהִי לְוַת דָוִד וּנְפַל עַל אַרְעָא וּסְגִיד:


On the third day, behold, a man came from Saul’s camp, with his clothes torn and earth thrown upon his head. And when he came to David, he fell to the ground and bowed down.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:3)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ דָוִד אֵי מִדֵין אַתְּ אָתֵי וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ מִמַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אִשְׁתְּזֵיבִית:


David said to him, “Where are you coming from?” And he said to him, “I have escaped from the camp of Israel.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:4)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ דָוִד מֶה הֲוָה פִתְגָמָא חַוִי כְעַן לִי וַאֲמַר דְאַפִּיךְ עַמָא מִן קְרָבָא וְאַף סַגִי נְפַל מִן עַמָא וְאִתְקְטָלוּ וְאַף שָׁאוּל וִיהוֹנָתָן בְּרֵיהּ מִיתוּ:


And David said to him, “What was the situation? Tell me now.” And he said, “The people have retreated from the battle, and many of the people also have fallen and have been killed; and Saul and his son Jonathan are also dead.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:5)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְעוּלֵמָא דִמְחַוֵי לֵיהּ אֵיכְדִין יְדַעְתָּ אֲרֵי מִית שָׁאוּל וִיהוֹנָתָן בְּרֵיהּ:


Then David said to the young man who told him, “How do you know that Saul and his son Jonathan are dead?”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:6)


וַאֲמַר עוּלֵמָא דִמְחַוֵי לֵיהּ אִתְעֲרָעָא אִתְעַרְעִית בְּטוּרָא דְגִלְבּוֹעַ וְהָא שָׁאוּל סָמִיךְ עַל מוּרְנִיתֵיהּ וְהָא רְתִיכַיָא וּמַשִׁרְיַת פְּרָשַׁיָא אַדְבְּקוֹהִי:


And the young man who told him said, “By chance I happened to be on Mount Gilboa; and there was Saul leaning upon his spear. And behold, the chariots and an army of horsemen overtook him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:7)


וְאִתְפְּנֵי לַאֲחוֹרוֹהִי וַחֲזָנִי וּקְרָא לִי וַאֲמַרִית הָא אֲנָא:


And when he turned behind him, he saw me and called me. And I said, ‘Here I am.’


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:8)


וַאֲמַר לִי מַן אַתְּ וַאֲמַרִית לֵיהּ עֲמַלְקָאָה אֲנָא:


And he said to me, ‘Who are you?’ And I said to him, ‘I am an Amalekite.’


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:9)


וַאֲמַר לִי קוּם כְּעַן עֲלַי וּקְטוֹלָנִי אֲרֵי אַחֲדַנִי רְתִיתָא אֲרֵי כָל עוֹד נַפְשִׁי בִּי:


And he said to me, ‘Stand beside me and kill me, for trembling has seized me. And even now my soul is within me.’


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:10)


וְקָמִית עֲלוֹהִי וּקְטַלְתֵּיהּ אֲרֵי יָדַעְנָא אֲרֵי לָא יְחֵי בָתַר דִי נְפַל וּנְסֵיבִית כְּלִילָא דִי עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וְטוֹטַפְתָּא דְעַל דַרְעֵיהּ וְאַיְתֵיתִינוּן לְוַת רִבּוֹנִי הַלְכָא:


So I stood beside him and killed him, because I knew that he could not live after he had fallen. And I took the crown which was on his head and the phylactery which was on his arms, and I have brought them here to my lord.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:11)


וְאַתְקֵיף דָוִד בִּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי וּבַזְעִנוּן וְאַף כָּל גַבְרַיָא דְעִמֵיהּ:


Then David took hold of his clothes and tore them. And so did all the men who were with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:12)


וּסְפָדוּ וּבְכוֹ וְצָמוּ עַד רַמְשָׁא עַל שָׁאוּל וְעַל יְהוֹנָתָן בְּרֵיהּ וְעַל עַמָא דַייָ וְעַל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי אִתְקְטָלוּ בְּחַרְבָּא:


And they mourned and wept and fasted until evening for Saul and for Jonathan his son and for the people of the LORD and for the House of Israel, because they had been killed by the sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:13)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְעוּלֵמָא דִמְחַוֵי לֵיהּ אֵי מִדֵין אַתְּ וַאֲמַר בַּר גְבַר גִיוֹר עֲמַלְקָאָה אֲנָא:


And David said to the young man who told him, “Where are you from?” And he said, “I am the son of a proselyte, an Amalekite man.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:14)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ דָוִד אֵיכְדֵין לָא דְחַלְתָּא לְאוֹשָׁטָא יְדָךְ לְחַבָּלָא יַת מְשִׁיחָא דַיָי:


David said to him, “How is it you were not afraid to stretch out your hand to harm the LORD’s anointed?”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:15)


וּקְרָא דָוִד לְחַד מֵעוּלֵמַיָא וַאֲמַר קְרַב שְׁלוֹט בֵּיהּ וּמְחָהִי וְקַטְלֵיהּ:


Then David called one of the young men and said, “Draw near, overpower him.” And he struck him down and killed him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:16)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ דָוִד חוֹבַת קְטוֹלָךְ תְּהֵי בְּרֵישָׁךְ אֲרֵי פּוּמָךְ אַסְהִיד בָּךְ לְמֵימַר אֲנָא קַטְלֵית יַת מְשִׁיחָא דַיָי:


And David said to him, “The guilt of killing you be upon your head, for your own mouth has testified against you, saying, “I have killed the LORD’s anointed.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:17)


וַאֲלָא דָוִד יַת אַלְיָא הָדֵין עַל שָׁאוּל וְעַל יְהוֹנָתָן בְּרֵיהּ:


And David lamented with this lamentation over Saul and over Jonathan his son.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:18)


וַאֲמַר לְאַלְפָא בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה מֵיגַד בְּקַשְׁתָּא הָא הִיא כְתִיבָה עַל סִפְרָא דְאוֹרַיְתָא:


And he promised to teach the sons of Judah the bending of the bow, behold, it is written in the book of the Torah.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:19)


אִתְעַתַּדְתּוּן יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל בֵּית תּוֹקְפֵיכוֹן אִתְרְמֵיתוּן קְטִילִין אֵיכְדֵין אִתְקְטָלוּ גִיבָּרַיָא:


“You took your stand, O Israel. Upon your stronghold you were thrown, killed. How are the heroes killed!


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:20)


לָא תְחַווּן בְּגַת לָא תְבַסְרוּן בְּשׁוּקֵי אַשְׁקְלוֹן דִלְמָא יְחַדְיָן בְגַת פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי דִילְמָא יְדוּצוּן בְּגַת עֲרֵלַיָא:


Tell it not in Gath, announce it not in the streets of Ashkelon, lest the daughters of the Philistines rejoice, lest the daughters of the uncircumcised exult.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:21)


טוּרֵי גִלְבּוֹעַ לָא יֵחוֹת עֲלֵיכוֹן טַלָא וּמִטְרָא לָא תְהֵי בְּכוֹן עֲלָלָא כְּמִסַת דְיַעְבְדוּן מִנֵיהּ חֲלָתָא אֲרֵי תַמָן אִיתְבָּרוּ תְּרֵיסֵי גִיבָּרַיָא תְּרֵיסָא דְשָׁאוּל דִמְשִׁיחַ כִּדְבְמִשְׁחָא:


Ye mountains of Gilboa, let there be no dew or rain upon you, let there be no sufficient yield on you, that they make from it a dough offering. For there the heroes’ shields were broken, the shield of Saul, anointed as if with anointing oil.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:22)


מִדַם קְטִילִין מִתְּרַב גִבָּרִין גִירֵי קַשְׁתָּא דִיהוֹנָתָן לָא מְסַתַּחֲרִין לַאֲחוֹרָא וְחַרְבָּא דְשָׁאוּל לָא תַיְבָא רֵיקָן:


From the blood of the killed, from the fat of the heroes the arrows of Jonathan’s bow are not turning back and Saul’s sword is not returning empty.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:23)


שָׁאוּל וִיהוֹנָתָן דִרְחִימִין וַחֲבִיבִין בְּחַיֵיהוֹן וּבְמוֹתְהוֹן לָא אִתְפָּרָשׁוּ מֵעִמְהוֹן מִנִשְׁרַיָא קַלִילִין מֵאַרְיְוָתָא גִבָּרִין:


Saul and Jonathan, beloved and lovely in their life, and in their death not distinguished from their people; swifter than eagles and more heroic than lions.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:24)


בְּנַת יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל שָׁאוּל בְּכָאָה דַהֲוָה מַלְבִּישׁ לְכוֹן לְבוּשֵׁי צִבְעוֹנִין וּמוֹבִיל לְכוֹן תַּפְנוּקִין וּמַסִיק תִּיקוּנֵי דַהֲבָא עַל לְבוּשְׁכוֹן:


Ye daughters of Israel, weep over Saul, who clothed you with scarlet clothes and brought you delicacies and put golden ornaments on your clothes.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:25)


אֵיכְדֵין אִתְקְטְלוּ גִבָּרַיָא בְּגוֹ קְרָבָא יְהוֹנָתָן עַל בֵּית תּוּקְפָךְ אִתְקְטַלְתָּא:


How are the heroes shattered in the midst of the battle! Jonathan, you are killed on your stronghold!


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:26)


עָקַת לִי עֲלָךְ אֲחִי יְהוֹנָתָן חֲבִיבַת לִי לַחֲדָא מַפְרְשָׁא רְחִמְתָּךְ לִי מֵרְחִימַת תַּרְתֵּין נְשִׁין:


I am troubled for you, my brother Jonathan, you were very lovely for me! Your love was more distinguished for me than the love of my two wives.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/1:27)


אֵיכְדֵין אִתְקְטָלוּ גִבָּרַיָא וַאֲבָדוּ מָנֵי קְרָבָא:


How are the heroes shattered, and the utensils of war perished!”



## Chapter 2

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:1)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר כֵּן וּשְׁאֵיל דָוִד בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ לְמֵימָר הַאֶסַק בַּחֲדָא מִקִרְוַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה וַאֲמַר יְיָ לֵיהּ סַק וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְאָן אֲסַק וַאֲמַר לְחֶבְרוֹן:


After this David inquired of the speech of the LORD, “Shall I go up to one of the cities of the House of Judah?” And the LORD said to him, “Go up.” David said, “To which shall I go up?” And He said, “To Hebron.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:2)


וּסְלִיק תַּמָן דָוִד וְאַף תַּרְתֵּין נְשׁוֹהִי אֲחִינוֹעַם דְמִיִזְרְעֶאל וַאֲבִיגַיִל אִתַּת נָבָל דְמִכַּרְמְלָא:


So David went up there, and his two wives also, Ahinoam, who was of Jezreel, and Abigail, the widow of Nabal, who was from Carmel.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:3)


וְגַבְרוֹהִי דְעִמֵיהּ אַסֵיק דָוִד גְבַר וֶאֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ וִיתָבוּ בְּקִרְוֵי חֶבְרוֹן:


And David brought up his men who were with him, every one with the people of his house. And they dwelt in the towns of Hebron.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:4)


וַאֲתוֹ גַבְרֵי שֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה וּמְשָׁחוּ תַמָן יַת דָוִד לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא עַל דְבֵית יְהוּדָה וְחַוִיאוּ לְדָוִד לְמֵימָר גַבְרֵי יָבֵשׁ גִלְעָד דִקְבַרוּ יַת שָׁאוּל:


And the men of Judah came and there they anointed David to be king over the House of Judah. And they told David, “It was the men of Jabesh gilead who buried Saul.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:5)


וּשְׁלַח דָוִד אִזְגַדִין לְוַת אֱנָשֵׁי יָבֵשׁ גִלְעָד וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן בְּרִיכִין אַתּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ דַעְבַדְתּוּן חִסְדָא הָדֵין עִם רִבּוֹנְכוֹן עִם שָׁאוּל וּקְבַרְתּוּן יָתֵיהּ:


So David sent messengers to the men of Jabesh gilead and said to them, “Blessed be you before the LORD, because you showed this loyalty to Saul your lord, and buried him!


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:6)


וּכְעַן יַעְבֵּד יְיָ עִמְכוֹן טִיבוּ וּקְשׁוֹט וְאַף אֲנָא אַעְבֵיד עִמְכוֹן טַבְתָא הָדָא דַעֲבַדְתּוּן פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין:


Now may the LORD show goodness and truth to you! And I will do good to you, because you have done this thing.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:7)


וּכְעַן יִתְקְפִן יְדֵיכוֹן וַהֲווֹ לְגַבְרִין גִבָּרִין אֲרֵי מִית רִבּוֹנְכוֹן שָׁאוּל וְאַף יָתִי מְשָׁחוּ דְבֵית יְהוּדָה לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא עֲלֵיהוֹן:


And now, let your hands be strong and be heroic men, because Saul your lord is dead and also because those of the House of Judah have anointed me to be king over them.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:8)


וְאַבְנֵר בַּר נֵר רַב חֵילָא דִי לְשָׁאוּל דְבַר יַת אִישׁ בּוֹשֶׁת בַּר שָׁאוּל וְאַעֲבִירֵיהּ לְמַחֲנָיִם:


Now Abner the son of Ner, commander of Saul’s army, had taken Ish bosheth the son of Saul, and brought him over to Mahanaim.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:9)


וְאַמְלְכֵיהּ עַל דְבֵית גִלְעָד וְעַל דְבֵית אָשֵׁר וְעַל דְבֵית יִזְרְעֶאל וְעַל דְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם וְעַל דְבֵית בִּנְיָמִן וְעַל דְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל כּוּלְהוֹן:


He had made him king over those of the House of Gilead, the House of Asher, the House of Jezreel, the House of Ephraim, and the House of Benjamin; in short over Israel in its entirety.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:10)


בַּר אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין אִישׁ בֹּשֶׁת בַּר שָׁאוּל כַּד מְלַךְ עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתַרְתֵּין שְׁנִין מְלַךְ בְּרַם דְבֵית יְהוּדָה הֲווֹ בָּתַר דָוִד:


Ish bosheth son of Saul was forty years old when he became king over Israel, and he reigned two years. But the house of Judah followed David.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:11)


וַהֲוָה מִנְיַין יוֹמַיָא דַהֲוָה דָוִד מַלְכָּא בְּחֶבְרוֹן עַל דְבֵית יְהוּדָה שְׁבַע שְׁנִין וְשִׁיתָּא יַרְחִין:


And the number of years that David was king in Hebron over those of the House of Judah was seven years and six months.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:12)


וּנְפַק אַבְנֵר בַּר נֵר וְעַבְדֵי אִישׁ בּוֹשֶׁת בַּר שָׁאוּל מִמַחֲנַיִם לְגִבְעוֹן:


And Abner the son of Ner and the servants of Ish bosheth the son of Saul went out from Mahanaim to Gibeon,


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:13)


וְיוֹאָב בַּר צְרוּיָה וְעַבְדֵי דָוִד נְפַקוּ וְעַרְעוּנוּן עַל בְּרֵיכָתָא דִבְגִבְעוֹן כַּחֲדָא וּשְׁרוֹ אִילֵין עַל בְּרֵיכָתָא מִיכָּא וְאִילֵין עַל בֵּרֵיכָתָא מִיכָּא:


whereas Joab the son of Zeruiah and the servants of David went out and met them at the pool of Gibeon. And they sat down there, the one on the one side of the pool and the other on the other side of the pool.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:14)


וַאֲמַר אַבְנֵר לְיוֹאָב יְקוּמוּן כְּעַן עוּלֵימַיָא וִיחַיְיכוּן קֳדָמָנָא וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב יְקוּמוּן:


And Abner said to Joab, “Let the young men arise and play before us.” And Joab said, “Let them arise.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:15)


וְקָמוּ וַעֲבַרוּ בְּמִנְיָן תְּרֵי עֲסַר מִדְבֵית בִּנְיָמִן וּמִדְאִישׁ בֹּשֶׁת בַּר שָׁאוּל וּתְרֵי עֲסַר מֵעַבְדֵי דָוִד:


Then they arose and passed over by number, twelve from the House of Benjamin and from Ish bosheth the son of Saul, and twelve from the servants of David.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:16)


וְאַתְקִיפוּ גְבַר בְּרֵישׁ חַבְרֵיהּ וְחַרְבֵּיהּ בִּסְטַר חַבְרֵיהּ וּנְפָלוּ כַּחֲדָא וּקְרָא לְאַתְרָא הַהוּא אַחֲסָנַת קְטִלַיָא דִי בְּגִבְעוֹן:


And each caught his opponent by the head and, with his sword in the place of his opponent’s loins, they were killed together. And he called that place “Inheritance of the Killed Ones”, which is in Gibeon.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:17)


וַהֲוָה קְרָבָא תַּקִיף עַד לַחֲדָא בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וְאִתַּבַּר אַבְנֵר וֶאֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָם עַבְדֵי דָוִד:


And the battle was extremely fierce that day. And Abner and the men of Israel were shattered before the servants of David.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:18)


וַהֲווֹ תַמָן תְּלָתָא בְּנֵי צְרוּיָה יוֹאָב וַאֲבִישַׁי וַעֲשָׂהאֵל וַעֲשָׂהאֵל קַלִיל בְּרִיגְלוֹהִי כַּחֲדָא מִן טָבַיָא דִי בְּחַקְלָא:


And the three sons of Zeruiah were there, Joab, Abishai, and Asahel. Now Asahel was as swift of foot as a gazelle which is in the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:19)


וּרְדַף עֲשָׂהאֵל בָּתַר אַבְנֵר וְלָא אִיתְפְּנֵי לְמֵיזַל עַל יַמִינָא אוֹ עַל סְמָלָא מִבָּתַר אַבְנֵר:


And Asahel pursued Abner, and he did not turn, to go either to the right hand or to the left from following Abner.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:20)


וְאִיתְפְּנִי אַבְנֵר בַּתְרוֹהִי וַאֲמַר הַאַתְּ דֵין עֲשָׂהאֵל וַאֲמַר אֲנָא:


Then Abner turned to look behind him and said, “Is it you, Asahel?” And he said, “It is I.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:21)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אַבְנֵר אִיתְפְנֵי לָךְ עַל יְמִינָךְ אוֹ עַל סְמָלָךְ וְאֵיחוּד לָךְ חַד מֵעוּלֵימַיָא וְסַב לָךְ יַת זְרָזֵיהּ וְלָא אָבָה עֲשָׂהאֵל לְמֵיזַר מִבַּתְרוֹהִי:


Abner said to him, “Turn aside to your right hand or to your left, and seize one of the young men, and take his belt.” But Asahel would not depart from following him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:22)


וְאוֹסִיף עוֹד אַבְנֵר לְמֵימָר לַעֲשָׂהאֵל זוּר לָךְ מִבַּתְרַי לָמָה אֶקְטְלִינָךְ וְאֶרְמִינָךְ לְאַרְעָא וְאֵיכְדִין אֶזְקוֹף אַפֵּי וְאֵיחָמֵי לְיוֹאָב אָחוּךְ:


And Abner said again to Asahel, “Depart from me, for your own sake! Why should I kill you and smite you to the ground? How then could I lift up my face and show it to your brother Joab?”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:23)


וְלָא אָבָא לְמֵיזַר וּמְחָהִי אַבְנֵר בַּאֲחוֹרֵי מוּרְנִיתָא בִּסְטַר יַרְכֵיהּ וּנְפָקַת מוּרְנִיתָא מֵאֲחוֹרוֹהִי וּנְפַל תַּמָן וּמִית בְּאַתְרֵיהּ וַהֲוָה כָּל דְאָתֵי לְאַתְרָא דִנְפַל תַּמָן עֲשָׂהאֵל וּמִית וְקַיְמִין:


But he did not want to depart. Then Abner smote him in the place of his loins with the back of his spear, so that the spear came out at his back. And he fell there, and died on the place where he was. And all who came to the place where Asahel had fallen and died, stood still.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:24)


וּרְדָפוּ יוֹאָב וַאֲבִישַׁי בָּתַר אַבְנֵר וְשִׁמְשָׁא עָלַת וְאִינוּן מְטוֹ עַד גִבְעַת אַמְתָא דִי עַל אַפֵּי גִיחַ אוֹרַח מַדְבְּרָא דְגִבְעוֹן:


But Joab and Abishai pursued Abner. And as the sun was going down, they came to the hill of The Cubit, which lies before Giah, on the road to the wilderness of Gibeon.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:25)


וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִן בָּתַר אַבְנֵר וַהֲווֹ לְסִיעָא חָד וְקָמוּ עַל רֵישׁ רָמָתָא חֲדָא:


And the sons of Benjamin gathered themselves together behind Abner, and became one company. They took their stand upon the top of one hill.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 2:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/2:26)


וּקְרָא אַבְנֵר לְיוֹאָב וַאֲמַר הַלְאַפְרֵשׁ תִּקְטוֹל חַרְבָּא הֲלָא יְדַעְתָּא אֲרֵי מְרִירָא תְהֵי בְסוֹפָא וְעַד אֵימָתַי לָא תֵימַר לְעַמָא לְמִתַּב מִבָּתַר אֲחֵיהוֹן:


Then Abner called to Joab, “Shall the sword kill for ever? Do you not know that it will be bitterness in the end? How long are you not saying to your people to turn back from behind their brethren?”

When Abner came with twenty men to David at Hebron, David made a feast for Abner and the men who were with him.

## Chapter 4

When the son of Saul heard that Abner had died at Hebron, his hands became inactive and all Israel was terrified.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/4:2)

Now there were two men, commanders – heads – of two camps with the son of Saul. The name of the one was Baanah, and the name of the other Rechab, sons of Rimmon, a man of Benjamin from Beeroth – for Beeroth also is reckoned to the House of Benjamin.

And the men of Beeroth fled to Gittaim, and have been inhabitants there to this day.

Jonathan, the son of Saul, had a son suffering in both his feet. He had been five years old when the news about Saul and Jonathan came from Jezreel. And his nurse took him up and turned away, but in her haste to flee he fell and became cripple. And his name was Mephibosheth.

וְאִינוּן אֲתוֹ עַד גוֹ בֵיתָא כְּזַבְּנֵי חִטִין וּמְחוֹהִי בִּסְטַר יַרְכֵיהּ וְרֵכַב וּבַעֲנָה אֲחוֹהִי אִשְׁתֵּיזָבוּ:


And behold, they came into the midst of the house as buyers of wheat, and they struck him in the place of his loins. Then Rechab and Baanah his brother escaped.

וַאֲתוֹ לְבֵיתָא וְהוּא שָׁכִיב עַל עַרְסֵיהּ בְּאִדְרוֹן בֵּית מִשְׁכְּבֵיהּ וּמְחוֹהִי וְקַטְלוֹהִי וְאַעְדִיאוּ יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ וּנְסִיבוּ יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ וַאֲזַלוּ בְּאוֹרַח מֵישְׁרָא כָּל לַיְלְיָא:


When they came to the house, as he was sleeping on his bed in his bedchamber, they struck him and killed him and cut off his head. And they took his head and went the way of the valley all night.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/4:8)


וְאַיְתִיאוּ יַת רֵישׁ אִישׁ בּוֹשֶׁת לְוַת דָוִד לְחֶבְרוֹן וַאֲמַרוּ לְמַלְכָּא הָא רֵישׁ אִישׁ בֹּשֶׁת בַּר שָׁאוּל בְּעֵיל דְבָבָךְ דִבְעָא לְמִקְטְלָךְ וַעֲבַד יְיָ לְרִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא פּוּרְעֲנוּתָא יוֹמָא הָדֵין מִשָׁאוּל וּמִזַרְעֵיהּ:


They brought the head of Ish bosheth to David at Hebron. And they said to the king, “Behold the head of Ish bosheth, the son of Saul, your enemy, who sought to kill you. The LORD took revenge on Saul and on his seed this day for my lord the king.”

But David answered Rechab and Baanah his brother, the sons of Rimmon who was from Beeroth, “As the LORD is the existing One, who has redeemed my life out of every affliction,


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 4:10

when one told me, saying, ‘Behold, Saul is dead,’ and he was like one bringing good news in the sight of his own soul, I seized him and killed him at Ziklag, while he was imagining me to give him a present for his good news.

אַף אֲרֵי גַבְרִין רַשִׁיעַיָא קְטַלוּ יַת גְבַר זַכַּאי בְּבֵיתֵיהּ עַל שִׁווּיֵה וּכְעַן הֲלָא אִתְבַּע יַת דְמֵיהּ מִיֶדְכוֹן וַאֲפַלֵי יַתְכוֹן מִן אַרְעָא:


How much more, when wicked men have killed an innocent man in his own house upon his couch, shall I not now require his blood from your hand, and remove you from the land?”

And David commended his young men, and they killed them. They cut off their hands and feet, and hanged them beside the pool at Hebron. But they took the head of Ish bosheth, and buried it in the tomb of Abner at Hebron.

Then all the tribes of Israel came to David at Hebron, and said, “Behold us, we are your kin and flesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 5:2

Already in times past, when Saul was king over us, it was you that was going out and coming in at the head of Israel. And the LORD said to you, ‘You shall take care of my people Israel, and you shall be king over Israel.’”

And David inquired of the speech of the LORD, saying, “Shall I go up against the Philistines? Wilt Thou surrender them into my hand?” And the LORD said to David, “Go up, for I will certainly surrender the Philistines into your hand.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/5:20)


וַאֲתָא דָוִד בְּמֵישַׁר פְּרָצִים וּמְחָנוּן תַּמָן דָוִד וַאֲמַר תְּבַר יְיָ יַת בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵי קֳדָמַי כְּתִבּוּר מָאן דַחֲסַף דְמָלֵי מַיָין עַל כֵּן קְרָא שְׁמֵיהּ דְאַתְרָא הַהוּא מֵישַׁר פְרָצִים:


And David came to the valley of Perazim, and David struck them down there. And he said, “The LORD has broken my haters before me, like the breaking of a vessel of clay full of water.” Therefore he called the name of that place the Valley of Perazim.

וּשְׁבַקוּ תַּמָן יַת טַעֲוַתְהוֹן וְאוֹקֵידִינוּן דָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי:


And they had left their idols there, and David and his men burnt them.

And the Philistines came up yet again, and spread out in the Valley of the Heroes.

וּשְׁאֵיל דָוִד בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ וַאֲמַר לָא תֵיסַק אִסְתַּחֵר לַאֲחוֹרֵיהוֹן וּתְהַךְ לְהוֹן מִקָבֵיל אִילָנַיָא:


And when David inquired of the speech of the LORD, he said, “You shall not go up. Go around to their rear, and go toward them opposite the trees.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/5:24)

And about the time you hear the sound of an outcry in the tops of the trees, then you will be strengthened, for then the angel of the LORD will have gone out to succeed before you to kill among the camp of the Philistines.

And David did as the LORD commanded him, and killed the Philistines from Geba to the entrance of Gezer.



## Chapter 6

And again David went on gathering all the chosen men of Israel, thirty thousand.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/6:2)


וְקָם וַאֲזַל דָוִד וְכָל עַמָא דִי עִמֵיהּ מִקִרְוַיָא מִדְבֵית יְהוּדָה לְאַסָקָא מִתַּמָן יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ דְאִיתְקְרֵי שְׁמָא שְׁמָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת דִשְׁכִינְתֵּה שַׁרְיָא עֵיל מִן כְּרוּבַיָא עֲלוֹהִי:


And David arose and went with all the people who were with him from the cities of the House of Judah, to bring up from there the ark of the LORD which is called by the Name, the Name of the LORD *Sebaoth*, whose Presence dwells above the cherubim.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/6:3)


וַאֲחִיתוּ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ בְּעֶגְלָתָא חַדְתָּא וּנְטָלוֹהִי מִבֵּית אֲבִינָדָב דִי בְּגִבְעֲתָא וְעֻזָא וְאַחְיוֹ בְנֵי אֲבִינָדָב מַדְבְּרִין יַת עֶגְלָתָא חַדְתָּא:


And they carried the ark of the LORD upon a new cart, and carried it out of the house of Abinadab, which was on the hill. And Uzzah and Ahio, sons of Abinadab, were leading the new cart.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 6:4

They carried it out of the house of Abinadab, which was on the hill. They were going along with the ark of the LORD, that is, Ahio was walking before the ark.

And David returned to bless the people of his house. But Michal the daughter of Saul came out to meet David, and said, “How the king of Israel honoured himself today, exposing himself today before the eyes of his servants’ handmaids, as one of the immoral people who strips and exposes himself.”

And David said to Michal, “Before the LORD, who chose me above your father, and above all his house, to command me to be king over the people of the LORD, over Israel – I praised before the LORD.

וּמַן כְּעַמָךְ כְּיִשְׂרָאֵל עַמָא חַד בְּחִיר בְּאַרְעָא דַאֲזַלוּ שְׁלוּחִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְמִפְרַק לֵיהּ לְעָם וּלְשַׁוָאָה לֵיהּ שׁוּם וּלְמֶעְבַּד לְכוֹן רַבְרְבָן וַחֲסִינָן עַד דִי עָלוּ לַאֲרַע בֵּית שְׁכִנְתָּךְ דִיהַבְתְּ לְהוֹן מִן קֳדָם עַמָךְ דִי פְרַקְתָּא לָךְ מִמִצְרַיִם גוֹיִם וֵאלֹהָיו:


And who is like thy people Israel, one choice people on earth, whom those who were sent from before the LORD went to save [to become] a people for Him, and to make for Him a name, and to do for you great and strong things, until they came to the land of the House of thy Presence, which Thou hast given to them from before thy people, which Thou hast saved for Thyself from Egypt,


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:24)


וְאַתְקֵינְתָּא לָךְ יַת עַמָךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָמָךְ לְעַם עַד עָלְמָא וְאַתְּ יְיָ הֱוֵיתָא לְהוֹן לֵאלָהָא:


And Thou didst establish for Thyself thy people Israel to be a people before Thyself for ever; and Thou, O LORD, didst become their God.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:25)


וּכְעַן יְיָ אֱלֹהִים פִּתְגָמָא דִי מַלֵלְתָּא עַל עַבְדָךְ וְעַל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ אָקִים עַד עָלְמָא וֶעֱבֵיד כְּמָא דִי מַלֵלְתָּא:


And now, O LORD God, confirm for ever the word which Thou hast spoken concerning thy servant and concerning his house, and do as Thou hast spoken.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:26)


וְיִסְגֵי שְׁמָךְ עַד עָלְמָא לְמֵימַר יְיָ צְבָאוֹת אֱלֹהִים עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבֵית עַבְדָךְ דָוִד יְהֵי מְתַקֵן קֳדָמָךְ:


And thy name will be magnified for ever, saying, ‘The LORD *Sebaoth* is God over Israel’; and the house of thy servant David will be established before Thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:27)


אֲרֵי אַתְּ יְיָ צְבָאוֹת אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל חֲוֵיתָא לְעַבְדָךְ לְמֵימָר מַלְכוּ אֲקַיֵם לָךְ עַל כֵּן הֲוָת בְּלִבָּא דְעַבְדָךְ לְצַלָאָה קֳדָמָךְ יַת צְלוֹתָא הָדֵין:


For thou, O LORD *Sebaoth*, the God of Israel, hast told thy servant, saying, ‘I will establish you a kingdom’; therefore it was in thy servant’s heart to pray this prayer before Thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 7:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:28)


וּכְעַן יְיָ אֱלֹהִים אַתְּ הוּא יְיָ וּפִתְגָמָךְ אִינוּן קְשׁוֹט וּמַלֵלְתָּא עַל עַבְדָךְ יַת טַבְתָא הָדָא:


And now, O LORD God, Thou art God, and thy words are true; and Thou hast spoken all this goodness to thy servant.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 7:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/7:29)


וּכְעַן שְׁרֵי וּבְרִיךְ יַת בֵּית עַבְדָךְ לְמֶהֱוֵי לְעַלְמָא קֳדָמָךְ אֲרֵי אַתְּ יְיָ אֱלֹהִים מַלֵלְתָּא וּמִבִּרְכָתָךְ יִתְבָּרְכוּן בָּתֵּי עַבְדָךְ צַדִיקַיָא לַעֲלָם:


And now, be willing to bless the house of thy servant, that it may be for ever before Thee. For Thou, O LORD God, hast spoken, with thy blessing shall the houses of thy servants the righteous ones be blessed in eternity.”



## Chapter 8

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:1)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר כֵּן וּמְחָא דָוִד יַת פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְתַבְּרִינוּן וּנְסִיב דָוִד יַת תִּקוּן אַמְתָא מִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי:


After this David struck down the Philistines and shattered them. David took the arrangement of the cubit out of the hand of the Philistines.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:2)


וּמְחָא יַת מוֹאֲבָאֵי וּמְשַׁח לְהוֹן בְּעַדְבָא רָמֵי יַתְהוֹן לְאַרְעָא וּמְשַׁח תְּרֵין חַבְלִין לְמִקְטַל וּמְלֵי חַבְלָא לַאֲחָאָה וַהֲווֹ מוֹאָבָא לְדָוִד לְעַבְדִין נַטְלֵי פְרָס:


And he struck down the Moabites, and measured them with the lot: he threw them to the ground and measured two lots to kill and filled one lot to be spared. And the Moabites were servants to David, bringing tribute.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:3)


וּמְחָא דָוִד יַת הַדַדְעֶזֶר בַּר רְחוֹב מַלְכָּא דְצוֹבָא כַּד אֲזַל לְאַשְׁנָאָה תְּחוּמֵיהּ בִּנְהַר פְּרָת:


And David struck down Hadadezer the son of Rehob, king of Zobah, as he went to change his border at the river Euphrates.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:4)


וַאֲסַר דָוִד מִנֵיהּ אֲלַף וּשְׁבַע מְאָה פְּרָשִׁין וְעַשְׂרִין אַלְפִין גְבַר רַגְלִי וַעֲקַד דָוִד יַת כָּל רְתִיכַיָא וְאַשְׁאַר מִנְהוֹן מְאָה רְתִיכִין:


And David captured from him a thousand and seven hundred horsemen, and twenty thousand foot soldiers. And David destroyed all the chariots, but left a hundred chariots.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:5)


וַאֲתוֹ אֱנַשׁ אֲרַם דַמֶשֶׂק לְמִסְעֹד לַהֲדַדְעֶזֶר מַלְכָּא דְצוֹבָה וּמְחָא דָוִד בַּאֲרָם עַשְׂרִין וּתְרֵין אַלְפִין גַבְרָא:


And when the men of Aram of Damascus came to help Hadadezer king of Zobah, David struck down twenty two thousand men of Aram.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:6)


וּמַנֵי דָוִד אִסְטַרְטִיגִין בַּאֲרַם דַמֶשֶׂק וַהֲווֹ אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם לְדָוִד לְעַבְדִין נַטְלֵי פְרָס וּפְרַק יְיָ יַת דָוִד בְּכָל אֲתַר דִי הֲלִיךְ:


Then David appointed praetors in Aram of Damascus, and the men of Aram became servants to David, bringing tribute. And the LORD saved David in whatever place he went.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:7)


וּנְסִיב דָוִד יַת שִׁלְטֵי דְדַהֲבָא דַהֲווֹ עַל עַבְדֵי הֲדַדְעֶזֶר וְאַיְתִינוּן לִירוּשְׁלֵם:


And David took the shields of gold which were [carried] by the servants of Hadadezer, and brought them to Jerusalem.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:8)


וּמִבֶּטַח וּמִבֵּרוֹתַי קִרְוֵי הֲדַדְעֶזֶר נְסִיב מַלְכָּא דָוִד נְחָשָׁא סַגִי לַחֲדָא:


And from Betah and from Beroth, cities of Hadadezer, King David took very much bronze.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:9)


וּשְׁמַע תּוֹעִי מַלְכָּא דַחֲמָת אֲרֵי מְחָא דָוִד יַת כָּל מַשִׁרְיַת הֲדַדְעֶזֶר:


When Toi king of Hamath heard that David had defeated the whole army of Hadadezer,


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:10)


וּשְׁלַח תּוֹעִי יַת יוֹרָם בְּרֵיהּ לְוַת מַלְכָּא דָוִד לְמִשְׁאַל לֵיהּ לִשְׁלָם וּלְבָרְכוּתֵיהּ עַל דְאַגִיחַ קְרָבָא בַּהֲדַדְעֶזֶר וְקַטְלֵיהּ אֲרֵי גְבַר עָבֵיד קְרָבִין עִם תּוֹעִי הֲוָה הֲדַדְעֶזֶר וְעִמֵיהּ הֲווֹ מָנֵי כַסְפָּא וּמָנֵי דַהֲבָא וּמָנֵי נְחָשָׁא:


Toi sent his son Joram to King David to inquire after his welfare and to bless him, because he had waged war against Hadadezer and killed him; for Hadadezer was a man often fighting battles against Toi. And he brought with him articles of silver, articles of gold, and articles of bronze.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:11)


אַף יַתְהוֹן אַקְדִישׁ מַלְכָּא דָוִד קֳדָם יְיָ עִם כַּסְפָּא וְדַהֲבָא דִי אַקְדִישׁ מִכָּל עַמְמַיָא דִי כַבֵּשׁ:


These also King David dedicated before the LORD, together with the silver and gold which he dedicated from all the nations he subdued,

מֵאֲרָם וּמִמוֹאָב וּמִבְּנֵי עַמוֹן וּמִפְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּמֵעֲמַלְקָאֵי וּמִבִּיזַת הֲדַדְעֶזֶר בַּר רְחוֹב מַלְכָּא דְצוֹבָה:


from Edom, Moab, the sons of Ammon, the Philistines, the Amalekites, and from the spoil of Hadadezer the son of Rehob, king of Zobah.

וּכְנַשׁ דָוִד מַשִׁרְיַן כַּד תַּב מִלְמִמְחֵי יַת אֲרָם בְּגֵיא מֶלַח תַּמְנַת עֲסַר אַלְפִין:


And David gathered armies, when he returned from striking down Aram in the Valley of Salt: eighteen thousand men.

וּמַנֵי בֶאֱדוֹם אִסְטַרְטִיגִין בְּכָל אֱדוֹם מַנֵי אִסְטַרְטִיגִין וַהֲווֹ כָל אֱדוֹם עַבְדִין לְדָוִד וּפְרַק יְיָ יַת דָוִד בְכָל אֲתַר דִי הֲלִיךְ:


And he appointed praetors in Edom; throughout all Edom he appointed praetors, and all the men of Edom became David’s servants. And the LORD saved David in whatever place he went.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/8:15)


וּמְלַךְ דָוִד עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַהֲוָה דָוִד עָבֵיד דִין דִקְשׁוֹט וְזָכוּ לְכָל עַמֵיהּ:


And David was king over all Israel. And David administered true justice and righteousness to all his people.

וְיוֹאָב בַּר צְרוּיָה מְמַנָא עַל חֵילָא וִיהוֹשָׁפָט בַּר אֲחִילוּד מְמַנָא עַל דָכְרָנַיָא:


Joab the son of Zeruiah was appointed over the army; and Jehoshaphat the son of Ahilud was appointed over the records.

וְצָדוֹק בַּר אֲחִיטוּב וַאֲחִימֶלֶךְ בַּר אֶבְיָתָר כַּהֲנַיָא וּשְׂרָיָה סַפְרָא:


And Zadok the son of Ahitub and Ahimelech the son of Abiathar were priests. And Seraiah was scribe.

And Benaiah the son of Jehoiada was appointed over the archers and over the slingers. And the sons of David were teachers.



## Chapter 9

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/9:1)

And David said, “Is there still any one left of the house of Saul, that I may show him goodness for Jonathan’s sake?”

And you shall till the land for him, you and your sons and your servants, and you shall bring it in that it may be food for your lord’s son and that he may be provided for. And Mephibosheth the son of your lord shall always eat at my table.” Now Ziba had fifteen sons and twenty servants.

## Chapter 10

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:1

After this the king of the sons of Ammon died, and Hanun his son reigned in his stead.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:2)

And David said, “I will show goodness to Hanun the son of Nahash, as his father has shown goodness to me.” So David sent his servants to console him concerning his father. And David’s servants came into the land of the sons of Ammon.

But the commanders of the sons of Ammon said to their lord Hanun, “Is David honouring your father, do you think that he has sent comforters to you? Has not David sent his servants to you to spy out the land and to examine it and to evaluate it?”

וּבְנֵי עַמוֹן חֲזוֹ אֲרֵי אַפָכוּ אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם וַעֲרָקוּ מִן קֳדָם אֲבִישַׁי וַאֲתוֹ לְקַרְתָּא וְתַב יוֹאָב מִלְוַת בְנֵי עַמוֹן וַאֲתָא לִירוּשְׁלֵם:


And when the sons of Ammon saw that the men of Aram retreated, they fled before Abishai, and entered the city. Then Joab returned from the sons of Ammon and came to Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:15)


וַחֲזוֹ אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם אֲרֵי אִתְבָּרוּ קֳדָם יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ כַּחֲדָא:


But when the men of Aram saw that they had been shattered before Israel, they gathered themselves together.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:16)


וּשְׁלַח הֲדַדְעֶזֶר וְאַפֵּיק יַת אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם דְמֵעִבַּר פְּרָת וַאֲתוֹ לְחֵילָם וְשׁוֹבַךְ רַב חֵילָא דְהֲדַדְעֶזֶר קֳדָמֵיהוֹן:


And Hadadezer sent, and brought out the men of Aram who were beyond the Euphrates. And they came to Helam, with Shobach the commander of the army of Hadadezer before them.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:17)


וְאִיתְחַוָא לְדָוִד וּכְנַשׁ יַת כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַעֲבַר יַת יַרְדְנָא וַאֲתוֹ לְחֵלָם וְסַדָרוּ אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם לְקֳדָמוּת דָוִד וְאַגִיחוּ קְרָבָא עִמֵיהּ:


And when it was told David, he gathered all Israel together, and crossed the Jordan, and came to Helam. And the men of Aram drew up against David and waged war with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:18)


וְאַפָּכוּ אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם מִן קֳדָם יִשְׂרָאֵל וּקְטַל דָוִד מֵאֲרָם שְׁבַע מְאָה רְתִיכִין וְאַרְבְּעִין אַלְפִין פָּרָשִׁין וְיַת שׁוֹבָךְ רַב חֵילֵיהּ מְחוֹ וּמִית תַּמָן:


And the men of Aram retreated before Israel; and David killed of Aram seven hundred chariots and forty thousand horsemen, and struck down Shobach the commander of their army, so that he died there.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:19)


וַחֲזוֹ כָל מַלְכַיָא עַבְדֵי הֲדַדְעֶזֶר אֲרֵי אִתְבָּרוּ קֳדָם יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַשְׁלִימוּ עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל וּפַלְחוּנוּן וּדְחִילוּ אֱנַשׁ אֲרָם לְמִפְרַק עוֹד יַת בְּנֵי עַמוֹן:


And when all the kings who were servants of Hadadezer saw that they had been shattered before Israel, they made peace with Israel, and became subject to them. So the men of Aram feared to save the sons of Ammon any more.



## Chapter 11

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:1)


וַהֲוָה לִזְמַן סוֹפָא דְשַׁתָּא לְעִדַן מִפַּק מַלְכַיָא וּשְׁלַח דָוִד יַת יוֹאָב וְיַת עַבְדוֹהִי עִמֵיהּ וְיַת כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְחַבִּילוּ יַת בְּנֵי עַמוֹן וְצָרוּ עַל רַבָּה וְדָוִד יָתֵיב בִּירוּשְׁלֵם:


And around the end of the year, the time when the kings go forth, David sent Joab and his servants with him, and all Israel; and they did damage to the sons of Ammon, and besieged Rabbah. But David remained in Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:2)


וַהֲוָה לְעִדַן רַמְשָׁא וְקָם דָוִד מֵעַל שִׁיווּיֵהּ וַהֲלִיךְ עַל אִגַר בֵּית מַלְכָּא וַחֲזָא אִתְּתָא מִשְׁתַּטְפָא עַל אִגְרָא וְאִתְּתָא שְׁפִירַת חֵזוּ לַחֲדָא:


And at the time of the evening, when David arose from his couch and walked upon the roof of the king’s house, he saw from the roof a woman bathing; and the woman was very beautiful of appearance.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:3)


וּשְׁלַח דָוִד וּשְׁאַל לְאִתְּתָא וַאֲמַר הֲלָא דָא בַּת שֶׁבַע בַּת אֱלִיעָם אִתַּת אוּרִיָה חִתָּאָה:


And David sent and inquired about the woman, and he said, “Is not this Bathsheba, the daughter of Eliam, the wife of Uriah the Hittite?”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:4)

So David sent messengers to lead her; and she came to him, and he slept with her, while she had been purifying herself from her uncleanness. Then she returned to her house.

Uriah said to David, “The ark, as well as Israel and Judah, dwell in booths, while my lord Joab and the servants of my lord are camping in the open field. Shall I then go to my house, to eat and to drink, and to lie with my wife? By your life, and the life of your soul, I will not do this thing.”

## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 12:1

וּשְׁלַח יְיָ יַת נָתָן לְוַת דָוִד וַאֲתָא לְוָתֵיהּ וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ תְּרֵין גַבְרִין הֲווֹ בְקַרְתָּא חָדָא חַד עֲתִּירָא וְחַד מִסְכֵּינָא:


And the LORD sent Nathan to David. He came to him, and said to him, “There were two men in a certain town, the one rich and the other poor.

The rich man had very large flocks and herds.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:3)


וּלְמִסְכֵּינָא לֵית כָּל מִדָעַם אֱלָהֵין אִמַרְתָּא חֲדָא זְעֵירָא דְזַבְּנָא וְקַיְימָהּ וּרְבַת עִמֵיהּ וְעִם בְּנוֹהִי כַּחֲדָא מִפִּתֵּיהּ אֲכָלַת וּמִכּוֹסֵיהּ שַׁתְיָא וּבְחֵיקֵיהּ שָׁכְבָא וַהֲוַת לֵיהּ כִּבְרַתָּא:


But the poor man had nothing, except for one little ewe lamb, which he had bought. And he raised her and she grew up together with him and with his sons: it used to eat of his morsel, and used to drink from his cup, and used to lie in his bosom. It was like a daughter to him.

Now there came a traveller to the rich man, and he thought it a pity that he should take one of his own flock or herd to prepare for the traveller who had come to him. So he took the poor man’s lamb, and prepared it for the man who had come to him.”

and I gave you your lord’s house, and your lord’s wives into your bosom, and I gave you the House of Israel and Judah. And if this were too little, I would add to you as much more.

וַחֲזָא דָוִד אֲרֵי עַבְדוֹהִי מְלַחֲשִׁין וּסְבַר דָוִד אֲרֵי מִית רַבְיָא וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְעַבְדוֹהִי הֲמִית רַבְיָא וַאֲמַרוּ מִית:


But when David saw that his servants were whispering, David perceived that the boy was dead. And David said to his servants, “The child is dead.” And they said, “He is dead.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:20)

Then David arose from the ground, and washed, and anointed himself, and changed his clothes. He went into the sanctuary of the LORD, and bowed down. He then went to his own house, and when he asked, they set food before him, and he ate.

וַאֲמַרוּ עַבְדוֹהִי לֵיהּ מַה פִתְגָמָא הָדֵין דַעֲבַדְתָּא עַד דְרַבְיָא קַיָם צַמְתָּא וּבְכֵית וְכַד מִית רַבְיָא קַמְתָּא וַאֲכַלְתְּ לַחְמָא:


Then his servants said to him, “What is this thing you have done? As long as the boy was alive, you fasted and wept; but when the boy died, you arose and ate bread.”

He said, “As long as the boy was alive, I fasted and wept, for I said, ‘Who knows? Perhaps mercy will be shown over me from before the LORD that the boy may live.’

And he brought forth the people who were in it, andsawed them with saws and iron picks and iron axes, and he dragged them through the streets. And thus he would do to all the cities of the sons of Ammon. Then David and all the people returned to Jerusalem.

And afterwards Absalom, David’s son, had a beautiful sister, whose name was Tamar. And Amnon, David’s son, loved her.

And it so distressed Amnon that he made himself ill because of his sister Tamar, for she was a virgin, and it was concealed in the sight of Amnon what to do.

And he said, “Why are you so miserable morning after morning, O son of the king? Will you not tell me?” Amnon said to him, “Tamar, the sister of my brother Absalom – I love her.”

And Jonadab said to him, “Lie down on your couch, and pretend to be ill. And when your father comes to see you, say to him, ‘Let my sister Tamar come and give me bread to eat, and prepare the meal in my sight, that I may see it, and eat it from her hand.’”

So Amnon lay down, and pretended to be ill. And when the king came to see him, Amnon said to the king, “Let my sister Tamar come now and mix two dumplings in my sight, that I may eat from her hand.

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:1)


וִידַע יוֹאָב בַּר צְרוּיָה אֲרֵי לִבָּא דְמַלְכָּא לְמִפַּק עַל אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


And Joab the son of Zeruiah perceived that it was in the king’s heart to go forth against Absalom.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:2)


וּשְׁלַח יוֹאָב לִתְקוֹעַ וּנְסֵיב מִתַּמָן אִתְּתָא חֲכִּמְתָּא וַאֲמַר לָהּ אִתְאַבְּלֵי כְעַן וּלְבוּשִׁי כְעַן לְבוּשֵׁי אֶבְלָא וְלָא תְשׁוּפִין מִשְׁחָא וּתְהֵא כְּאִתְּתָא דְנַן יוֹמִין סַגִיאִין מִתְאַבְּלָא עַל מֵיתָא:


And Joab sent to Tekoa, and took from there a wise woman, and said to her, “Pretend to be mourning, and put on mourning garments. Do not anoint yourself with oil, but behave like a woman who has been mourning many days for a dead one.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:3)


וְתַהֲכִין לְוַת מַלְכָּא וּתְמַלְלִין עִמֵיהּ כְּפִתְגָמָא הָדֵין וְשַׁוַי יוֹאָב יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא בְּפוּמָהּ:


And you shall go to the king and speak with him according to these words.” So Joab put the words in her mouth.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:4)


וַאֲמָרַת אִתְּתָא דְמִתְּקוֹעַ לְמַלְכָּא וּנְפָלַת עַל אַפָּהּ עַל אַרְעָא וּסְגִידַת וַאֲמָרַת פְּרוֹק מַלְכָּא:


When the woman, who was of Tekoa, said to the king, she fell on her face to the ground, and bowed down, and said, “Save, O king.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:5)


וַאֲמַר לָהּ מַלְכָּא מַה לִיךְ וַאֲמָרַת בְּקוּשְׁטָא אִתְּתָא אַרְמַלְתָּא אֲנָא וּמִית בַּעֲלִי:


And the king said to her, “What is your trouble?” She answered, “In truth, I am a widowed woman; my husband is dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:6)


וּלְאַמְתָךְ תְּרֵין בְּנִין וְאִתְנְצִיאוּ תַרְוֵיהוֹן בְּחַקְלָא וְלֵית דִמְשֵׁיזֵיב בֵּינֵיהוֹן וּמְחוֹ חַד יַת חַד וּקְטַל יָתֵיהּ:


And your handmaid had two sons, and the two of them quarrelled in the field. Since there was no one to save them from each other, the one struck the other and killed him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:7)


וְהָא קָמַת כָּל זַרְעִיתָא עַל אַמְתָךְ וַאֲמַרוּ הָבִי וְנִקְטוֹל יַת דִקְטַל אֲחוֹהִי וְנִקְטְלִינֵיהּ בְּחוֹבַת נַפְשָׁא דַאֲחוֹהִי דִקְטַל וּנְשֵׁיצֵי אַף יַת יָרוֹתָא וּבְעוֹ לְמִקְטַל יַת חַד יְחִידִי דְאִשְׁתְּאַר בְּדִיל דְלָא לְשַׁוָאָה לְבַעֲלֵי שׁוּם וּשְׁאָר עַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


And behold, the whole family has risen against your handmaid, and they said, ‘Give up the one who killed his brother, that we may kill him for the guilt concerning his brother’s life whom he killed. So we will also destroy the heir.’ They seek to kill my only one who is left in order to leave to my husband neither name nor remnant upon the face of the earth.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:8)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא לְאִתְּתָא אֱזִילִי לְבֵיתָךְ וַאֲנָא אֲפַקֵיד עֲלָךְ:


Then the king said to the woman, “Go to your house, and I will give orders concerning you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:9)


וַאֲמַרַת אִתְּתָא דְמִתְּקוֹעַ לְמַלְכָּא עֲלַי דִי לִי רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא חוֹבָא וְעַל בֵּית אַבָּא וּמַלְכָּא וְכוּרְסֵי מַלְכוּתֵיהּ זַכַּאי:


And the woman, who was of Tekoa, said to the king, “On me, mine be the guilt, my lord the king, and on my father’s house. Let the king and the throne of his kingdom be innocent.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:10)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא מַן דְיֵימַר לִיךְ מִלָא וְתַיְתִינֵיהּ לְוָתִי וְלָא יוֹסִיף עוֹד לְאַנְזָקָה בִיךְ:


The king said, “Whoever says a word to you, bring him to me, and he shall never harm you again.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:11)


וַאֲמַרַת יִדְכַּר כְּעַן מַלְכָּא מַה דִכְתִיב בִּסְפַר אוֹרַיְתָא דַייָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּדִיל לְאַסְגָאָה אוֹרְחָא קֳדָם גָאֵל דְמָא לְחַבָּלָא וְלָא יְשֵׁיצוֹן יַת בְּרִי וַאֲמַר קַיָם הוּא יְיָ אִם יִפּוֹל מִסְעַר רֵישׁ בְּרִיךְ לְאַרְעָא:


Then she said, “Let the king now remember what is written in the book of the law of the LORD your God, not to enlarge the way before the avenger of blood to do harm. So let them not finish off my son.” And he said, “As the LORD is the existing One, not one hair of your son’s head shall fall to the ground.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:12)


וַאֲמַרַת אִתְּתָא תְּמַלֵל כְּעַן אַמְתָךְ קֳדָם רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא פִתְגָמָא וַאֲמַר מַלֵילִי:


Then the woman said, “Let your handmaid now speak a word before my lord the king.” And he said, “Speak.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13)


וַאֲמַרַת אִתְּתָא וּלְמָה חֲשַׁבְתָּא כַּהֲדָא עַל עַמָא דַייָ וּמְמַלֵיל מַלְכָּא פִתְגָמָא הָדֵין כִּגְבַר חַיָב בְּדִיל דְלָא לְאַתָבָא מַלְכָּא יַת בִּדוּרֵיהּ:


And the woman said, “Why then have you planned such a thing against the people of the LORD – in speaking this word the king is like a guilty man – in order not to bring back the king’s banished one?


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:14)


אֲרֵי מִיתָא דְמָאִית וּכְמַיָא דְמִתְאַשְׁדִין לְאַרְעָא דְלָא אֶפְשַׁר לְהוֹן דְיִתּוֹסְפוּן כֵּן לֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְדַיָנָא דְקוּשְׁטָא לְקַבָּלָא מָמוֹן דִשְׁקַר וְדִמְחַשֵׁב מֵחְשְׁבָן בְּדִיל דְלָא לְבָדָרָא מִנֵיהּ בִּדוּרָא:


For the death of the dying is like the waters that are poured out on the ground so that it is impossible for them to be gathered together again; thus it is impossible for the court of truth to accept bribes and to devise plans not to scatter the banished one from him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15)


וּכְעַן דַאֲתֵיתִי לְמַלָלָא קֳדָם מַלְכָּא רִבּוֹנִי יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי דַחֲלוּנַנִי עַמָא וַאֲמַרַת אַמְתָךְ אֲמַלֵיל כְּעַן קֳדָם מַלְכָא מְאִים יַעְבֵּד מַלְכָּא יַת פִּתְגָמָא דְאַמְתֵיהּ:


And now that I have come to say this thing before my lord the king, because the people feared me, and your handmaid said, ‘I will speak before the king now; it may be that the king will do the request of his handmaid.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:16)


אֲרֵי יִשְׁמַע מַלְכָּא לְשֵׁיזָבָא יַת אַמְתֵיהּ מִיַד גַבְרָא לְשֵׁיצָאָה יָתִי וְיַת בְּרִי כַּחֲדָא מֵאַחֲסָנַת עַמָא דַיָי:


For the king will hear, and deliver his handmaid from the man who would destroy me and my son together from the heritage of the people of the LORD.’


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:17)


וַאֲמַרַת אַמְתָךְ יְהֵי כְעַן פִּתְגָמָא דְרִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא לְנִיחָא אֲרֵי כְּמַלְאָכָא דַייָ כֵּן רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא לְמִשְׁמַע טַב וּבִישׁ וּמֵימְרָא דַייָ אֱלָהָךְ יְהֵי בְסַעֲדָךְ:


And your handmaid thought, ‘Let the word of my lord the king set me at rest now, for like an angel of the LORD, so is my lord the king in discerning good and evil,’ and the speech of the LORD is in your aid.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:18)


וַאֲתֵיב מַלְכָּא וַאֲמַר לְאִתְּתָא לָא כְעַן תְּכַסֵי מִנִי פִתְגָמָא דַאֲנָא שְׁאֵל יָתִיךְ וַאֲמַרַת אִתְּתָא יְמַלֵיל כְּעַן רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא:


Then the king answered and said to the woman, “Now, do not hide from me anything I ask you.” And the woman said, “Let my lord the king speak now.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:19)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא הֲיַד יוֹאָב עִמָךְ בְּכָל דָא וַאֲתִיבַת אִתְּתָא וַאֲמַרַת חַיֵי נַפְשָׁךְ רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא אִם אִית לְמִסְטֵי לְיַמִינָא וְלִסְמָלָא מִכֹּל דִי מַלֵיל רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא אֲרֵי עַבְדָךְ יוֹאָב הוּא פַקְדַנִי וְהוּא שַׁוִי בְּפוּמָא דְאַמְתָךְ יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין:


The king said, “Is the hand of Joab with you in all this?” The woman answered, and said, “By your own life, my lord the king, one cannot turn to the right hand or to the left from all that my lord the king has said. It was your servant Joab who commanded me, and it was he who put all these words in the mouth of your handmaid.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:20)


בְּדִיל לְאַקָפָא יַת אַפֵּי פִתְגָמָא עֲבַד עַבְדָךְ יוֹאָב יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין וְרִבּוֹנִי חַכִּים כְּחָכְמַת מַלְאָכָא דַייָ לְמִידַע יַת כָּל דִי בְאַרְעָא:


In order to change the course of affairs your servant Joab did this thing. But my lord is wise like the wisdom of the angel of the LORD to know all that is on the earth.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:21)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא לְיוֹאָב הָא כְעַן עֲבָדִית יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין וֶאֱזֵיל אֲתֵיב יַת עוּלֵימָא יַת אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


Then the king said to Joab, “Behold now, I granted this thing. Go, bring back the young man, Absalom.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:22)


וּנְפַל יוֹאָב עַל אַפּוֹהִי לְאַרְעָא וּסְגִיד וּבָרִיךְ יַת מַלְכָּא וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב יוֹמָא דֵין יְדַע עַבְדָךְ אֲרֵי אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינָךְ רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא דַעֲבַד מַלְכָּא יַת פִּתְגָמָא דְעַבְדֵיהּ:


And Joab fell on his face to the ground, and bowed down, and blessed the king. And Joab said, “Today your servant knows that I have found favour in your sight, my lord the king, in that the king has granted the word of your servant.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:23)


וְקָם יוֹאָב וַאֲזַל לִגְשׁוּר וְאַיְתֵי יַת אַבְשָׁלוֹם לִירוּשְׁלֵם:


So Joab arose and went to Geshur, and brought Absalom to Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:24)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא יִסְתְּחַר לְבֵיתֵיהּ וְאַפֵּי לָא יֶחְזֵי וְאִסְתְּחַר אַבְשָׁלוֹם לְבֵיתֵיהּ וְאַפֵּי מַלְכָּא לָא חֲזָא:


And the king said, “Let him turn to his own house. He is not to see my face.” So Absalom turned to his own house, and did not see the king’s face.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:25)


וּכְאַבְשָׁלוֹם לָא הֲוָה גְבַר שַׁפִּיר בְּכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל לְשָׁבָּחָא לַחֲדָא מִפַּרְסַת רִגְלוֹהִי וְעַד מוֹחֵיהּ לָא הֲוָה בֵיהּ מוּמָא:


In all Israel there was no other handsome man so highly praised as Absalom. From the sole of his foot to his scalp there was no blemish in him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:26)


וּבְסַפָּרוּתֵיהּ יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ וַהֲוָה מִזְמַן עִדַן לְעִדַן דִמְסַפֵר אֲרֵי יַקִיר עֲלוֹהִי וּמְסַפֵּר לֵיהּ וּתְקַל יַת סְעַר רֵישֵׁיהּ מָאתָן סִלְעִין בְּמַתְקְלָא דְמַלְכָּא:


And when he shaved his head – on a yearly basis he shaved it; when it was heavy on him, he shaved it – he weighed the hair of his head: two hundred *sela* by the king’s weight.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:27)


וְאִתְיְלִידוּ לְאַבְשָׁלוֹם תְּלָתָא בְנִין וּבְרַתָּא חֲדָא וּשְׁמָהּ תָּמָר הִיא הֲוַת אִתְּתָא שַׁפִּירַת חֵיזוּ:


There were born to Absalom three sons, and one daughter whose name was Tamar. She was a woman beautiful of appearance.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:28)


וִיתֵב אַבְשָׁלוֹם בִּירוּשְׁלֵם תַּרְתֵּין שְׁנִין וְאַפֵּי מַלְכָּא לָא חֲזָא:


So Absalom dwelt two years in Jerusalem, without seeing the king’s face.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:29)


וּשְׁלַח אַבְשָׁלוֹם לְוַת יוֹאָב לְשַׁלְחָא יָתֵיהּ לְוַת מַלְכָּא וְלָא אֲבָה לְמֵיתֵי לְוָתֵיהּ וּשְׁלַח עוֹד תִּנְיָנוּת וְלָא אֲבָה לְמֵיתֵי:


Then Absalom sent for Joab to send him to the king, but Joab would not come to him. And he sent a second time, but he would not come.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:30)


וַאֲמַר לְעַבְדוֹהִי חֲזוֹ אַחְסַנְתֵּיהּ דְיוֹאָב דִסְמִיכָא לְדִילִי וְלֵיהּ תַּמָן סְעוֹרִין אֱזִילוּ וְאַדְלְקוּהָ בְּנוּרָא וְאַדְלִיקוּ עַבְדֵי אַבְשָׁלוֹם יַת אַחְסַנְתֵּיהּ בְּנוּרָא:


Then he said to his servants, “See Joab’s property is next to mine, and he has barley there; go and set it on fire.” So Absalom’s servants set the property on fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:31)


וְקָם יוֹאָב וַאֲתָא לְוַת אַבְשָׁלוֹם לְבֵיתָא וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ לְמָה אַדְלִיקוּ עַבְדָיִךְ יַת אַחְסַנְתָּא דִילִי בְּנוּרָא:


Then Joab arose and went to Absalom at his house, and said to him, “Why have your servants set the property which is mine on fire?”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:32)


וַאֲמַר אַבְשָׁלוֹם לְיוֹאָב הָא שְׁלָחִית לְוָתָךְ לְמֵימָר אִיתָא הַלְכָא וְאִשְׁלְחִינָךְ לְוַת מַלְכָּא לְמֵימַר לְמָה אָתֵיתִי מִגְשׁוּר טַב לִי עוֹד דַאֲנָא תַמָן וּכְעַן אֶחֱזֵי אַפֵּי מַלְכָּא וְאִם אִית בִּי חוֹבָא וְיִקְטְלִינַנִי:


Absalom said to Joab, “Behold, I sent for you, saying, ‘Come here, that I may send you to the king, saying, Why have I come from Geshur? It would be better for me if I were still there. And now, let me see the king’s face, and if there is guilt in me, let him kill me.’”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 14:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:33)


וַאֲתָא יוֹאָב לְוַת מַלְכָּא וְחַוִי לֵיהּ וּקְרָא לְאַבְשָׁלוֹם וַאֲתָא לְוַת מַלְכָּא וּסְגִיד לֵיהּ עַל אַפּוֹהִי עַל אַרְעָא קֳדָם מַלְכָּא וּנְשַׁק מַלְכָּא לְאַבְשָׁלוֹם:


Then Joab went to the king, and told him; and he called Absalom. So he came to the king, and bowed on his face to the ground before the king. And the king kissed Absalom.



## Chapter 15

וַהֲוָה מִבָּתַר כֵּן וַעֲבֵד לֵיהּ אַבְשָׁלוֹם רְתִיכִין וְסוּסְוַן וְחַמְשִׁין גַבְרָא רָדְפִין קֳדָמוֹהִי:


After this Absalom got himself chariots and horses, and fifty men to ride before him.

וּמַקְדִים אַבְשָׁלוֹם וְקָאֵים עַל כֵּיבַשׁ אוֹרַח תַּרְעָא וַהֲוֵי כָּל גַבְרָא דִיהֵי לֵיהּ דִין לְמֵיעַל לָקֳדָם מַלְכָּא לְמֵידַן וּקְרֵי אַבְשָׁלוֹם לֵיהּ וַאֲמַר אֵי מִדָא קַרְתָּא אַתְּ וַאֲמַר מֵחַד שִׁבְטַיָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַבְדָךְ:


And Absalom used to rise early and stand on the path of the gateway. And when any man had a suit to come before the king for jurisdiction, Absalom would call him, and say, “From what city are you?” And when he said, “Your servant is from one of the tribes of Israel,”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:3)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אַבְשָׁלוֹם חֲזֵי פִתְגָמָךְ תַּקְנִין וְיָאָן וְשָׁמַע לֵית לָךְ מִן קֳדָם מַלְכָּא:


Absalom would say to him, “See, your affairs are right and proper, but there is no man deputed before the king to hear you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:4)


וַאֲמַר אַבְשָׁלוֹם מַן יְמַנַנִי דַיָנָא בְּאַרְעָא וְקָדָמַי יֵיתִי כָּל גְבַר דִיהֵי לֵיהּ דִין וּמַצוּ וְאַדִינַנֵיהּ בִקְשׁוֹט:


And Absalom said, “Who would appoint me judge in the land? Then every man who had a suit or quarrel might come before me, and I would judge in truth.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:5)


וַהֲוָה כַּד קְרַב גַבְרָא לְמִסְגַד לֵיהּ וּמוֹשִׁיט יַת יְדֵיהּ וּמַתְקֵיף בֵּיהּ וּנְשִׁיק לֵיהּ:


And whenever a man approached to bow down for him, he would stretch out his hand, and take hold of him, and kiss him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:6)


וַעֲבַד אַבְשָׁלוֹם כְּפִתְגָמָא הָדֵין לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל דְאָתָן לְמִידַן לְוַת מַלְכָּא וְגָנֵב אַבְשָׁלוֹם יַת לִבָּא דֶאֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Thus Absalom did to all of Israel who were coming before the king for a suit; so Absalom stole the heart of the men of Israel.

And at the end of forty years Absalom said to the king, “Let me go now and fulfil my vow, which I have vowed to the LORD in Hebron.

For your servant vowed a vow during my stay in Geshur in Aram, saying, ‘If the LORD will indeed bring me back to Jerusalem, then I will worship before the LORD.’”

The king said to him, “Go in peace.” So he arose, and went to Hebron.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:10)


וּשְׁלַח אַבְשָׁלוֹם מְאַלְלִין בְּכָל שִׁבְטַיָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר כְּמִשְׁמָעֲכוֹן יַת קַל שׁוֹפָרָא וְתֵימְרוּן מְלַךְ אַבְשָׁלוֹם בְּחֶבְרוֹן:


But Absalom sent spies throughout all the tribes of Israel, saying, “When you hear the sound of the horn, then you shall say, ‘Absalom is king at Hebron!’”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 15:11

וְעִם אַבְשָׁלוֹם אֲזַלוּ מָאתָן גַבְרִין מִירוּשְׁלֵם זְמִינִין וְאָזְלִין לְתוּמֵיהוֹן וְלָא יָדְעִין כָּל מִדָעַם:


With Absalom went two hundred men from Jerusalem who were invited, and were going in their simplicity, and knew nothing at all.

וּשְׁלַח אַבְשָׁלוֹם יַת אֲחִיתוֹפֶל גִילוֹנָאָה מָלִיכָא דְדָוִד מִקַרְתֵּיהּ מִגִלֹה בְּדַבָּחוּתֵיהּ יַת דִבְחַיָא וַהֲוָה מַרְדָא תַּקִיף וְעַמָא אָזֵיל וְסַגִי עִם אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


And he sent for Ahithophel the Gilonite, David’s counsellor, from his city Giloh, while he was slaughtering the slaughterings. And the conspiracy grew strong, and the people with Absalom kept increasing.

And someone came telling David, saying, “The hearts of the men of Israel have turned after Absalom.”

So the king went forth, and all the people of his house with him. And the king left ten concubines to keep the house.

David said to him, “If you go on with me, you will be a burden for me.

But if you return to the city, and say to Absalom, ‘I will be your servant, O king. As I have been your father’s servant formerly, so now I will be your servant.’ So you will ruin Ahithophel’s counsel for me.

Are not Zadok and Abiathar the priests with you there? So whatever word you hear from the king’s house, you shall tell it to Zadok and Abiathar the priests.

## Chapter 16

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:1)


וְדָוִד עֲבַר זְעֵיר מֵרֵישׁ טוּרָא וְהָא צִיבָא עוּלֵימָא דִמְפִיבֹשֶׁת לָקֳדָמוּתֵיהּ וְזוֹג חֲמָרִין חֲשִׁיקִין וַעֲלֵיהוֹן מָאתָן גְרִיצָן דִלְחֵם וּמְאָה אִתְכְּלִין דְעִנְבִין יְבֵשִׁין וּמְאָה מָנָן דְבֵילָתָא וּגְרַב דַחֲמָר:


As David had passed a little beyond the top of the mountain, behold, Ziba the young man of Mephibosheth came to meet him, with a pair of asses saddled, and on them two hundred loaves of bread, a hundred clusters of dried grapes, a hundred *mines* of dried figs, and a skin of wine.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:2)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא לְצִיבָא מָה אִילֵין לָךְ וַאֲמַר צִיבָא חֲמָרַיָא לְבֵית מַלְכָּא לְמִרְכַּב וְלַחְמָא וּדְבֵילָתָא לְמֵיכַל עוּלֵימַיָא וְחַמְרָא לְמִשְׁתֵּי דִמְשִׁלְהֵי בְּמַדְבְּרָא:


And the king said to Ziba, “Why do you have these things?” And Ziba said, “The asses are for the king’s household to ride on, the bread and the dried figs for the young men to eat, and the wine for those who faint in the desert to drink.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:3)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא וְאָן בַּר רִבּוֹנָךְ וַאֲמַר צִיבָא לְמַלְכָּא הָא יָתֵיב בִּירוּשְׁלֵם אֲרֵי אֲמַר יוֹמָא דֵין יְתִיבוּן לִי בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל יַת מַלְכוּתָא דְאַבָּא:


And the king said, “And where is your lord’s son?” Ziba said to the king, “Behold, he remains in Jerusalem, for he said, ‘Today the House of Israel will give me back the kingdom of my father.’”

וַאֲמַר חוּשַׁי אַתְּ יְדַעְתָּא יַת אֲבוּךְ וְיַת גַבְרוֹהִי אֲרֵי גִבָּרִין אִינוּן וּמְרִירַן נַפְשָׁן אִינוּן כְּדוֹב תַּכֵּל בְּחַקְלָא וַאֲבוּךְ גְבַר עָבֵיד קְרָבִין וְלָא יְבִית יַת עַמָא:


And Hushai said, “You know that your father and his men are heroes, and that they are bitter of soul, like a bereaved bear in the field. Besides, your father is a man who wages wars and he will not spend the night with the people.
