[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:9)


הָא כְעַן הוּא טָמִיר בְּחַד מִן קוּמְצַיָא אוֹ בְּחַד מִן אַתְרַיָא וִיהֵי כְּמִיפֵל בְּהוֹן בְּקַדְמֵיתָא וְיִשְׁמַע דִי שְׁמַע וְיֵימַר הֲוַת מְחָתָא בְּעַמָא דְבָתַר אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


Behold, even now he has hidden himself in one of the pits, or in some of the other places. And when there are casualties among them at the first [attack], whoever hears it will say, ‘There has been a slaughter among the people who follow Absalom.’

וְהוּא אַף גְבַר גִבַּר דְלִיבֵּיהּ כְּלִבָּא דְאַרְיָא מִמַס יִמְסֵי אֲרֵי יְדַע כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי גִיבַּר אֲבוּךְ וְגַבְרִין גִבָּרִין דִי עִמֵיהּ:


And he, even [if he is] a heroic man whose heart is like the heart of a lion, will utterly melt, for all Israel knows that your father is a hero and that those who are with him are heroic men.

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:2)


וּשְׁלַח דָוִד יַת עַמָא תִּלְתָּא בְיַד יוֹאָב וְתִלְתָּא בְּיַד אֲבִישַׁי בַּר צְרוּיָה אֲחוּהִי דְיוֹאָב וְתִלְתָּא בְּיַד אִתַּי גִתָּאָה וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא לְעַמָא מִפַּק אֱפֹּק אַף אֲנָא עִמְכוֹן:


And David sent forth the people, one third by the hand of Joab, one third by the hand of Abishai the son of Zeruiah, Joab’s brother, and one third by the hand of Ittai the Gittite. And the king said to the people, “I myself will also go out with you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3)


וַאֲמַר עַמָא לָא תִפּוֹק אֲרֵי אִם מֶעֱרֹק נַעֲרוֹק לָא יְשַׁווּן עֲלָנָא לִבָּא וְאִם יִתְקַטְלוּן פַּלְגָנָא לָא יְשַׁווּן עֲלָנָא לִבָּא אֲרֵי כְעַן אַתְּ יָכִיל לְמִסְעַד כְּוָתָנָא עַסְרָא אַלְפִין וּכְעַן טַב אֲרֵי תְצַלֵי עֲלָנָא מִקַרְתָּא לְמִסְעַד:


But the people said, “You shall not go out. For if we finally retreat, they will pay no attention to us. And if half of us are killed, they will not pay attention to us either. For now, you are able to assist like ten thousand of us. And now, it is better that you pray for us from the city in order to assist.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:4)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן מַלְכָּא דְתַקִין בְּעֵינֵיכוֹן אַעְבֵּיד וְקָם מַלְכָּא עַל כֵּיבַשׁ אוֹרַח תַּרְעָא וְכָל עַמָא נְפָקוּ לְמָאתָן וּלְאַלְפִין:


The king said to them, “Whatever seems right in your sight I will do.” So the king stood on the path of the gateway, while all the people went out by hundreds and by thousands.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:5)


וּפַקֵיד מַלְכָּא יַת יוֹאָב וְיַת אֲבִישַׁי וְיַת אִתַּי לְמֵימַר אִסְתַּמָרוּ לִי בְּעוּלֵימָא בְאַבְשָׁלוֹם וְכָל עַמָא שְׁמָעוּ כַּד פַּקֵיד מַלְכָּא יַת כָּל רַבְרְבַיָא עַל עֵיסַק אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


And the king ordered Joab and Abishai and Ittai, “Deal gently for my sake with the young man, with Absalom.” And all the people heard when the king gave orders to all the commanders about the sake of Absalom.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:6)


וּנְפַק עַמָא לְחַקְלָא לִקֳדָמוּת יִשְׂרָאֵל וַהֲוַת קְרָבָא בְּחוּרְשָׁא דְבֵית אֶפְרָיִם:


So the people went out into the field against Israel. And the battle took place in the forest of the House of Ephraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:7)


וְאִתַּבָּרוּ תַמָן עַם יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָם עַבְדֵי דָוִד וַהֲוַת תַּמָן מְחָתָא רַבְּתָא בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא עַסְרִין אַלְפִין:


And the people of Israel were shattered there before the servants of David, and the stroke was great on that day, twenty thousand men.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:8)


וַהֲווֹ תַמָן עַבְדֵי קְרָבָא מִתְבַּדְרִין עַל אַפֵי כָל אַרְעָא וְאַסְגִיאַת חַיַת חוּרְשָׁא לְקַטָלָא בְעַמָא מִדְקָטִילַת חַרְבָּא בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


There the wagers of war were scattered over the face of all the country; so the beasts of the forest killed more people than the sword killed that day.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:9)


וְאִתְעֲרַע אַבְשָׁלוֹם קֳדָם עַבְדֵי דָוִד וְאַבְשָׁלוֹם רָכִיב עַל כּוּדַנְתָּא וְעָלַת כּוּדַנְתָּא תְּחוֹת סוֹכָא דְבוּטְמָא רַבָּא וְאִתְאֲחַד רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּבוּטְמָא וְאִתְּלֵי בֵּין שְׁמַיָא וּבֵין אַרְעָא וְכוּדַנְתָּא דִי תְּחוֹתוֹהִי עֲבָרַת:


And Absalom accidentally came in front of the servants of David. Now Absalom was riding upon a mule, and the mule went under the branch of a great terebinth, and his head was caught fast in the terebinth. And he was left hanging between heaven and earth, while the mule that was under him went on.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:10)


וַחֲזָא גַבְרָא חָד וְחַוֵי לְיוֹאָב וַאֲמַר הָא חֲזֵיתִי יַת אַבְשָׁלוֹם תְּלִי בְּבוּטְמָא:


And a certain man saw it, and told Joab, “Behold, I saw Absalom hanging in a terebinth.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:11)


וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב לְגַבְרָא דִמְחַוֵי לֵיהּ וְהָא חֲזֵיתָא וּמָא דֵין לָא מְחִיתָהוּ תַמָן וּרְמִיתָהִי לְאַרְעָא וַעֲלֵי פּוֹן לְמִתַּן לָךְ עֲסַר סִלְעִין דִכְסַף וְאִסְפַּנְקִי חֲדָא:


Joab said to the man who was telling him, “What, you saw him! Why then did you not strike him there and throw him to the ground? I would have given you about ten *selas* of silver and one girdle.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:12)


וַאֲמַר גַבְרָא לְיוֹאָב וְלוּ אַנָא תָּקֵל עַל כַּפֵּי אֲלַף סִלְעִין דִכְסַף לָא אוֹשִׁיט יְדֵי בְּבַר מַלְכָּא אֲרֵי קֳדָמָנָא פַּקֵיד מַלְכָּא יָתָךְ וְיַת אֲבִישַׁי וְיַת אִתַּי לְמֵימַר אִסְתַּמָרוּ לִי בְעוּלֵימָא בְּאַבְשָׁלוֹם:


But the man said to Joab, “Even if I were feeling in my hands the weight of a thousand *selas* of silver, I would not stretch out my hand against the king’s son, for before us the king commanded you and Abishai and Ittai, ‘Deal gently for my sake with the young man, with Absalom.’


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:13)


אוֹ פּוֹן עֲבָדִית בְּנַפְשִׁי שְׁקַר וְכָל מִדַעַם לָא יִתְכַּסָא מִן מַלְכָּא וְאַתְּ תִּתְעַתַּד מִקָבֵיל:


Or if I had dealt treacherously by myself, and there would be nothing hidden from the king, then you would have set yourself on the opposite side.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:14)


וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב הֲלָא מִבְּכֵין אֵישָׁרַי קֳדָמָךְ וּנְסִיב תְּלָתָא גַסְסִין בִּידֵיהּ וּקְבָעִינוּן בְּלִבָּא דְאַבְשָׁלוֹם וְעַד כְּעַן קַיָם בְּגוֹ בוּטְמָא:


And Joab said, “Shall I not now begin before you?” And he took three darts in his hand, and thrust them into the heart of Absalom, while he was still alive in the middle of the terebinth.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:15)


וְאִסְתַּחָרוּ עַסְרָא עוּלֵמַיָא נַטְלֵי זְיָנֵיהּ דְיוֹאָב וּמְחוֹ יַת אַבְשָׁלוֹם וְקַטְלוֹהִי:


And ten young men, Joab’s armour bearers, surrounded Absalom and struck him, and killed him.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:16)


וּתְקַע יוֹאָב בְּשׁוֹפָרָא וְתַב עַמָא מִלְמִרְדַף בָּתַר יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי מְנַע יוֹאָב יַת עַמָא:


Then Joab blew the trumpet, and the people came back from pursuing Israel, for Joab restrained the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:17)


וּנְסִיבוּ יַת אַבְשָׁלוֹם וּרְמוֹ יָתֵיהּ בְּחוּרְשָׁא לְגוֹ קוּמְצָא רַבָּה וַאֲקִימוּ עֲלוֹהִי דְגוֹר אַבְנִין רַב לַחֲדָא וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל אַפָּכוּ גְבַר לִקְרְווֹהִי:


And they took Absalom, and threw him into a great pit in the forest, and raised over him a very great heap of stones. And all Israel retreated, every one to his own town.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:18)


וְאַבְשָׁלוֹם נְסִיב וַאֲקֵים לֵיהּ בְּחַיוֹהִי יַת קָמָתָא דִי בְּמֵישַׁר מַלְכָּא אֲרֵי אֲמַר לֵית לִי בַר קַיָם בְּדִיל לְאַדְכָּרָא שְׁמִי וּקְרָא לְקָמָתָא עַל שְׁמֵיהּ וּקְרָא לָהּ אַתְרָא דְאַבְשָׁלוֹם עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין:


Now Absalom in his lifetime had taken and set up for himself the pillar which is in the King’s Valley, for he said, “I have no living son to keep my name in remembrance.” He called the pillar after his own name, and it is called Absalom’s Place to this day.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:19)


וַאֲחִימַעַץ בַּר צָדוֹק אֲמַר אֶרְהוּט כְּעַן וַאֲבַסַר יַת מַלְכָּא אֲרֵי אִתְפְּרַע לֵיהּ יְיָ מִיַד בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי:


Then said Ahimaaz the son of Zadok, “Let me run, and carry tidings to the king that the LORD has taken vengeance for him from the hand of his enemies.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:20)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יוֹאָב לָא גְבַר כְּשַׁר לְבַסָרָא אַתְּ יוֹמָא דֵין וּתְבַסֵר בְּיוֹם אוֹחֲרָן וְיוֹמָא הָדֵין לָא תְבַסֵר אֱלָהֵין דְבַּר מַלְכָּא מִית:


And Joab said to him, “You are not the right man to carry tidings this day, but you may carry tidings another day. On this day you have no other tidings but that the king’s son is dead.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:21)


וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב לְכוּשִׁי אֱזֵיל חַוֵי לְמַלְכָּא דַחֲזֵיתָא וּסְגִיד כּוּשִׁי לְיוֹאָב וּרְהַט:


Then Joab said to Cushi, “Go, tell the king what you have seen.” And Cushi bowed to Joab, and ran.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:22)


וְאוֹסִיף עוֹד אֲחִימַעַץ בַּר צָדוֹק וַאֲמַר לְיוֹאָב וִיהֵי מָה אֶרְהוֹט כְּעַן אַף אֲנָא בָּתַר כּוּשִׁי וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב לְמָא דְנַן אַתְּ רָהִיט בְּרִי וְלָךְ לֵית בְּסוֹרָא מִתְיְהַב:


Then Ahimaaz the son of Zadok said again to Joab, “Come what may, let me also run after Cushi.” And Joab said, “Why will you run, my son? There are no tidings to be given.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:23)


וִיהֵי מָא בִכְדֵין אֶרְהוּט וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ רְהוּט וּרְהַט אֲחִימַעַץ בְּאוֹרַח מֵישְׁרָא וּקְדִים יַת כּוּשִׁי:


“Come what may, let me run.” And he said, “Run.”And Ahimaaz ran by the way of the valley, and outran Cushi.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:24)


וְדָוִד יָתֵיב בֵּין תְּרֵין תַּרְעַיָא וְהַלִיךְ סַכְוָאָה עַל אִגַר תַּרְעָא עַל שׁוּרָא וּזְקֵף יַת עֵינוֹהִי וַחֲזָא וְהָא גַבְרָא רָהִיט בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי:


Now David was sitting between the two gates, and the watchman went up to the roof of the gate, up to the wall. And when he lifted up his eyes and looked, behold, a man running alone.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:25)


וּקְרָא סַכְוָאָה וְחַוֵי לְמַלְכָּא וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא אִם בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי הוּא בְּסוֹרְתָא בְּפוּמֵיהּ וַאֲזַל מֵיזַל וְקָרֵיב:

And the king was deeply shocked, and went up to the chamber over the gate, and wept. And thus he said, while he went, “O my son Absalom, my son, my son Absalom! Would I had died instead of you and you were alive today! O Absalom, my son, my son!”

by loving those who hate you and hating those who love you. For you have made it clear today that commanders and servants do not exist for you, for today I perceive that it would be precious to you, if Absalom were alive and all of us were dead today.

וְהָא כָּל אֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲתָן לְוַת מַלְכָּא וַאֲמַרוּ לְמַלְכָּא מָא דֵין כַּסְיוּךְ מִנָנָא אֲחָנָא אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה וְאַעְבָּרוּ יַת מַלְכָּא וְיַת אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ יַת יַרְדְנָא וְכָל גַבְרֵי דָוִד עִמֵיהּ:


And behold, all the men of Israel came to the king and said to the king, “Why have our brethren the men of Judah held you away from us, and brought the king and the people of his house over the Jordan, and all David’s men with him?”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 19:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:43)


וַאֲתֵיב כָּל אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה עַל אֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי קָרִיב לִי מַלְכָּא מִדִילָךְ וּלְמָה דְנַן תְּקוֹף לָךְ עַל פִתְגָמָא הָדֵין הֲמֵיכַל אֲכִילְנָא מִנִכְסֵי מַלְכָּא אִם מַתְּנָא מַנֵי לָנָא:


All the men of Judah answered the men of Israel, “Because the king is more near of kin to me than to you. Why then are you angry over this matter? Have we eaten at all from the property of the king? Or has he portioned out any gift to us?”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 19:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:44)


וַאֲתֵיב אֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל יַת אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה וַאֲמַר עַסְרָא חוּלָקִין אִית לִי בְּמַלְכָּא וְאַף בְּדָוִד אֲנָא רָעֵינָא מִנָךְ וּמָא דֵין אַשְׁטְתַנִי וְלָא הֲוָה פִתְגָמִי קַדְמוּתָא לִי לַאֲתָבָא יַת מַלְכִּי וּתְקֵיף פִּתְגַם אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה מִפִּתְגַם אֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And the men of Israel answered the men of Judah, and said, “I have ten shares in the king. And I have more pleasure in David than you. Why then did you despise me? And was not my word to bring back my king first?” But the words of the men of Judah were fiercer than the words of the men of Israel.



## Chapter 20

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:1)


וְתַמָן אִתְעַרַע גְבַר רַשִׁיעָא וּשְׁמֵיהּ שֶׁבַע בַּר בִּכְרִי גַבְרָא מִשִׁבְטָא דְבֵית בִּנְיָמִן וּתְקַע בְּשׁוֹפָרָא וַאֲמַר לֵית לָן חוּלַק בְּדָוִד וְלָא אַחְסָנָא לָנָא בְּבַר יִשַׁי גְבַר לְקִרְווֹהִי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Now there happened to be there a wicked man, whose name was Sheba, the son of Bichri, from the tribe of the House of Benjamin. And he blew the trumpet, and said, “We have no portion in David, four and we have no inheritance in Jesse’s son. Every man to his city, O Israel!”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:2)


וּסְלִיק כָּל אֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִבָּתַר דָוִד לְמֵיזַל בָּתַר שֶׁבַע בַּר בִּכְרִי וֶאֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה לָווּ עִם מַלְכְּהוֹן מִן יַרְדְנָא וְעַד יְרוּשְׁלֵם:


So all the men of Israel withdrew from David to go after Sheba the son of Bichri. But the men of Judah joined their king from the Jordan to Jerusalem.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:3)


וַאֲתָא דָוִד לְבֵיתֵיהּ לִירוּשְׁלֵם וּדְבַר מַלְכָּא יַת עֲסַר נְשִׁין לְחֵינָן דִי שְׁבַק לְמִטַר בֵּיתָא וּמַנִינָן בְּבֵית מַטָרָא וּמְסוֹבַר לְהֵן וּלְוַתְהֶן לָא עַל וַהֲוָאָה נְטִירָן וּמִתְקַרְיָן עַד יוֹם מוּתְהֵן אַרְמְלָן דְבַעְלֵיהֶן קַיָם:


And David came to his house at Jerusalem. And the king took the ten concubine wives whom he had left to guard the house, and put them in a house under guard. And he used to provide for them, but did not go in to them. So they were guarded, and they were called until the day of their death “widows of a living husband”.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:4)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא לַעֲמָשָׂא כְּנוֹשׁ קֳדָמַי יַת אֱנַשׁ יְהוּדָה תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין וְאַתְּ הָכָא קוּם:


Then the king said to Amasa, “Gather before me the men of Judah within three days, and stand here yourself.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:5)


וַאֲזַל עֲמָשָׂא לְמִכְנַשׁ יַת דְבֵית יְהוּדָה וְאוֹחַר מִן זִמְנָא דַאֲמַר לֵיהּ:


So Amasa went to gather those of the House of Judah, but he delayed beyond the set time which he had said to him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/20:6)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לַאֲבִישַׁי כְּעַן יַבְאֵישׁ לָנָא שֶׁבַע בַּר בִּכְרִי מִן אַבְשָׁלוֹם אַתְּ דְבַר יַת עַבְדֵי רִבּוֹנָךְ וּרְדַף בַּתְרוֹהִי דִלְמָא יַשְׁכַּח לֵיהּ קִרְוִין כְּרִיכָן וְיָעִיק לָנָא:


And David said to Abishai, “Now, Sheba the son of Bichri might do us more harm than Absalom. You must take your lord’s servants, and pursue him, lest he find himself fortified cities, and oppress us.”

וּנְפַקוּ בַּתְרוֹהִי גַבְרֵי יוֹאָב וְקַשָׁתַיָא וְקַלְעֲיָא וְכָל גִבָּרַיָא וּנְפַקוּ מִירוּשְׁלֵם לְמִרְדַף בָּתַר שֶׁבַע בַּר בִּכְרִי:


And the men of Joab, and the archers and slingers, and all the heroes went out after him. They went out from Jerusalem to pursue Sheba the son of Bichri.

אִינוּן עִם אַבְנָא רַבְּתָא דִי בְגִבְעוֹן וַעֲמָשָׂא אֲתָא לְאַפֵּיהוֹן וְיוֹאָב אֲסִיר מְזָרֵיז לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וַעֲלוֹהִי אִסְפַּנְקֵי חֶרֶב מְזָרְזָא עַל חַרְצֵיהּ בִּנְדָנָהּ וְהוּא אָזִיל וּפְסָע:


When they were at the great stone which is in Gibeon, Amasa came towards them. Now Joab was girded, wearing his clothes, and over them was a belt with a sword in its sheath girded upon his loins. And he went striding on.

And Joab said to Amasa, “Is is well with you, my brother?” And Joab took hold of Amasa’s beard with his right hand in order to kiss him.

וַעֲמָשָׂא לָא אִסְתְּמַר בְּחַרְבָּא דִי בְיַד יוֹאָב וּמְחָהִי בָהּ בִּסְטַר יַרְכֵיהּ וּשְׁפַךְ מֵיעוֹהִי לְאַרְעָא וְלָא תְנָא לֵיהּ וּמִית וְיוֹאָב וַאֲבִישַׁי אֲחוֹהִי רְדַף בָּתַר שֶׁבַע בַר בִּכְרִי:


So Amasa did not take heed of the sword which was in Joab’s [left] hand. And he struck him with it in the place of his loins, and shed his bowels to the ground – he did not need to repeat it – and he died. Then Joab and Abishai his brother pursued Sheba the son of Bichri.

And one of Joab’s men was standing by him, and said, “Whoever favours Joab, and whoever is for David, let him go after Joab.”

And Amasa lay wallowing in his blood in the middle of the road. And when the man saw that all the people stopped, he turned Amasa aside out of the road into the field, and threw clothes over him, since any one who came by saw him and stopped.

Joab answered, “Far be it, far be it from me, that I should ruin and destroy!

The matter is not so, for a man from the hill country of Ephraim, called Sheba the son of Bichri, has lifted up his hand against the king, against David. Give up him alone, and I will go away from the city.” And the woman said to Joab, “Behold, his head shall be thrown to you over the wall.”

Then the woman went to all the people in her wisdom. And they cut off the head of Sheba the son of Bichri, and threw it out to Joab. So he blew the trumpet, and they dispersed from the city, every man to his own town. And Joab returned to Jerusalem to the king.

Now there was a famine in the days of David for three years, year after year. And David sought mercy from before the LORD, and the LORD said, “It is because of Saul and because of the house of those deserving to be killed, because he killed the Gibeonites.”

So the king called the Gibeonites and said to them – now the Gibeonites were not of the sons of Israel, but of the remnant of the Amorites; although the sons of Israel had sworn to them, Saul had sought to kill them in his zeal for the sons of Israel and Judah.

And David praised in a prophetic spirit before the LORD with the words of this praise song, concerning all the days that the LORD saved Israel from the hand of all their enemies, and also David from the sword of Saul.

And he said, “The LORD is my strength and my trust and the one who rescues me.

אֱלָהִי דְאִתְרְעֵי בִי קָרֵבְנִי לִדְחַלְתֵּיהּ תּוּקְפֵי דְמִן קֳדָמוֹהִי מִתְיְהַב לִי תְּקוֹף וּפוּרְקָן לְאִתְגַבָּרָא עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי רוֹחֲצַנִי דְעַל מֵימְרֵיהּ אֲנָא רָחִיץ בְּעִדַן עָקָא מֵגִין עָלַי מִבַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי וַאֲמַר לַאֲרָמָא קַרְנִי בְּפוּרְקָנֵיהּ סוּמְכָנִי דַהֲוָה מֵימְרֵיהּ סְמַךְ לִי כַּד הֲוֵיתִי עָרִיק מִן קֳדָם רָדְפַי פּוּרְקָנִי מִבַּעֲלֵי דְבָבִי וְאַף מִיַד כָּל חֲטוֹפִין שֵׁיזִיב יָתִי:


My God, who chose me and drew me near to his fear. My strength, from before whom strength and salvation were given to me to overpower my enemies. My trust, on whose speech I trust in times of distress, shielding me against my enemies; He promised to raise my horn in his salvation. My support, whose speech was supporting me, when I was fleeing before my pursuers. My salvation from my enemies, and He saved me even from the hand of all robbers.”

and the LORD repaid me according to my innocence, according to my purity before his speech.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/22:26)

Abraham, who was found loyal before Thee – therefore, Thou hast increasingly acted loyally with his seed. Isaac, who was perfect in thy fear – therefore, Thou hast perfected the speech of thy pleasure with him.

Jacob, who was walking in purity before Thee – Thou hast chosen his sons from all the peoples, Thou hast separated his seed from all the rubbish. Pharaoh and the Egyptians, who were planning plans against thy people – Thou hast confused them according to their plans.

For by your speech I multiply armies, by the speech of my God I subdue all strong fortified cities,

Thou wilt save me from the strife of the nation. Thou wilt appoint me head of the nations; a nation that I do not know will be subjected to me.

Sons of the nations will perish, and they will come trembling out of their palaces.

Therefore, on account of the miraculous salvation that Thou didst for thy people, they gave thanks and said, ‘Existing is the LORD, and blessed be the Strong One, from before Whom strength and salvation was given to us, and exalted be God, the strength of our salvation.’

The God who took vengeance for me, and scattered under me the nations that were rising to harm me.

He saved me from my haters, He made me overpower those who were rising to harm me, He will save me from Gog and the armies of the robbing nations that are with him.

Therefore, I will give thanks before Thee, O LORD, among the nations, and to thy name I will say praise.

[He who] gives great salvation with his king, and shows goodness to his Anointed One, to David and to his seed for ever.”



## Chapter 23

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/23:1)


וְאִילֵין פִּתְגָמֵי נְבוּאַת דָוִד דְאִתְנַבִּי לְסוֹף עַלְמָא לְיוֹסֵי נֶחֱמְתָא דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיתֵי אֲמַר דָוִד בַּר יִשַׁי וַאֲמַר גַבְרָא דִמְרַבָּא לְמַלְכּוּ מְשִׁיחָא בְּמֵימָר אֱלָהֵיהּ דְיַעֲקֹב וְתַקִין לְמִימְנֵי בְּחֵיךְ מְבוֹסַם תּוּשְׁבַּחְתֵּיהּ דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


And these are the prophetic words of David, which he prophesied for the end of the world, for the days of consolation which are to come: David the son of Jesse said, and the saying of the man dignified to the kingdom, anointed by the Speech of Jacob’s God, and right to convey with a sweet palate the praises of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/23:2)


אֲמַר דָוִד בְּרוּחַ נְבוּאָה דַייָ אֲנָא מְמַלֵיל אִלֵין וּפִתְגָמֵי קוּדְשֵׁיהּ בְּפוּמֵי אֲנָא סָדַר:


David said, “By a prophetic spirit from before the LORD I am speaking these things, and his holy words I am arranging in my mouth.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 23:3

אֲמַר דָוִד אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל עֲלַי מַלֵיל תַּקִיפָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְשַׁלִיט בִּבְנֵי אֱנָשָׁא קוּשְׁטָא דָאִין אֲמַר לְמַנָאָה לִי מַלְכָּא דְהוּא מְשִׁיחָא דַעֲתִיד דִיקוּם וְיִשְׁלוֹט בִּדְחַלְתָּא דַיָי:


David said, “The God of Israel spoke about me, the Strong One of Israel, Who is Ruler among the sons of men, judging the truth. He said to appoint me king, that is, the Messiah who is to rise and will rule in the fear of the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 23:4

טוּבֵיכוֹן צַדִיקַיָא עֲבַדְתּוּן לְכוֹן עוֹבָדִין טָבִין דְאַתּוּן עֲתִידִין לְאַנְהָרָא בְּזֵיהוֹר יְקָרֵיהּ כִּנְהוֹר צַפְרָא דְאָזֵיל וְתַקִין וּכְשִׁימְשָׁא דַעֲתִיד לְאַנְהָרָא כְּזֵיהוֹר יְקָרֵיהּ עַל חַד תְּלַת מְאָה אַרְבְּעִין וּתְלָתָא כִּנְהוֹר שִׁבְעַת כּוֹכְבַיָא שִׁבְעַת יוֹמַיָא יַתִּיר מִכְּדֵין תִּתְרַבּוּן וְיֵיטַב לְכוֹן דַהֲוֵיתוֹן מֵחַמְדִין לִשְׁנֵי נֶחָמָתָא דְאַתְיָן הָא כְּאִכָּרָא דִמְסוֹבַר בִּשְׁנֵי בַּצוֹרְתָּא דְיֵחוֹת מִיטְרָא עַל אַרְעָא:


Blessed are you, righteous! You have done good deeds for yourselves, so that you are to shine in your glorious splendour, like the light of the dawn that grows brighter, and like the sun which is to shine in its glorious splendour, 343 times more in the light of the seven days. More than this you will be dignified, and it shall be good to you who are longing for the years of consolation to come. Behold, like a farmer who is hoping in dry years that rain will come down upon the land.”

אֲמַר דָוִד יַתִּירָא מִבְכֵין בֵּיתִי קֳדָם אֵל אֲרֵי קַיָים עֲלָם קַיֵים לִי לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכוּתִי קַיָימָא כְּמָא דְקַיָימִין סִידְרֵי בְּרֵאשִׁית וּנְטִירָא לְעַלְמָא דְאָתֵי אֲרֵי כָּל צָרְכִי וְכָל בָּעוּתִי קֳדָמוֹהִי מִתְעַבְּדִין בְּכֵן כָּל מַלְכוּ לְקִבְלֵיהּ עוֹד לָא תִתְקַיֵים:


David said, “More than this is my house before El, for He has established an eternal covenant for me, that my kingdom shall be lasting, as the order of creation are lasting, and secured for the world to come. Because all my needs and all my prayer are fulfilled before Him, therefore, every kingdom still opposite will not be established.

וְרַשִׁיעַיָא עָבְדֵי חֶטְאָה דָמָן לְכֻבִּין דִבְמִפַּקְהוֹן רַכִּיכִין לְמִקְטַף וְכַד אֱנַשׁ חַיֵס עֲלֵיהוֹן וְשָׁבֵיק לְהוֹן אָזְלִין וְתַקְפִין עַד דְלֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְמִקְרָב לְהוֹן בְּיָד:


And the wicked, doing sin, resemble thorn bushes, which are in their budding soft to cut off. But when a man spares them, and leaves them, they will grow stronger, until it is impossible to approach them by hand.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/23:7)


וְאַף כָּל אֱנַשׁ דִמְשָׁרֵי לְמִיקְרַב בְּחוֹבִין אָזְלִין וְתַקְפִין עֲלוֹהִי עַד דְחָפִין לֵיהּ כַּלְבוּשׁ דְפַרְזְלָא דְלָא יָכְלִין לֵיהּ בַּאָעֵי מוּרְנִין וְרוֹמָחִין בְּכֵין לֵית פּוּרְעֲנוּתְהוֹן בְּיַד אֱנַשׁ אֱלָהֵין בְּאֶשְׁתָּא עֲתִידִין לְאִתּוֹקָדָא יְתּוֹקְדוּן בְּאִתְגְלָאָה בֵּית דִינָא רַבָּא לְמִתַּב עַל כּוּרְסֵי דִינָא לְמֵידַן יַת עַלְמָא:


And thus is every man, too, who begins to approach sins: they will grow stronger over him, until they cover, like an iron garment, him whom they could not overpower with the wood of spears and javelins. Therefore, their punishment will not be by a man’s hand, but by the fires which are to be kindled. They will be kindled in the revelation of the High Court, [which is] to sit on the throne of judgment, to judge the world.”

These are the names of the heroes who were with David, the hero, head of the armies, sitting on the throne of judgment, and all the prophets and elders surrounding him; anointed with the holy anointing oil, chosen and pampered, beautiful in looks and handsome in appearance, wise in wisdom and intelligent in council, a hero in heroism, head of the heroes; he is prepared with sets of armour, going out as the champion, and victorious in battle; and wields his spear against eight hundred slain in one time.

## Chapter 24

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:1)


וְאוֹסִיף רוּגְזָא דַייָ לְמִתְקַף בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְגָרֵי יַת דָוִד בְּהוֹן לְמֵימַר אֱזִיל מְנֵי יַת יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיַת דְבֵית יְהוּדָה:


Again the anger of the LORD was strong against Israel, and he incited David against them, saying, “Go, count Israel and those of the House of Judah.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:2)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא לְיוֹאָב רַב חֵילָא דְעִמֵיהּ הֲלֵיךְ כְּעַן בְּכָל שִׁבְטָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל מִדָן וְעַד בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע וּמְנֵי יַת עַמָא וְאִידַע יַת חֻשְׁבַּן עַמָא:


So the king said to Joab, the commander of the army, who was with him, “Go now through all the tribes of Israel, from Dan to Beer sheba, and count the people, that I may know the number of the people.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:3)


וַאֲמַר יוֹאָב לְמַלְכָּא וְיוֹסֵף יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ עַל עַמָא כְּאִלֵין וּכְאִלֵין מְאָה זִמְנִין וְעֵינֵי רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא חָזָן וְרִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא לְמָא אִתְרְעֵי בְּפִתְגָמָא הָדֵין:


But Joab said to the king, “May the LORD your God add to the people a hundred times as many as they are, while the eyes of my lord the king still see it. But my lord the king, why does he delight in this thing?”

So they went through all the land. At the end of nine months and twenty days they came to Jerusalem.

And Joab gave the calculation of the number of people to the king: Israel had eight hundred thousand heroic men, who drew the sword, and the men of Judah had five hundred thousand.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:10)


וְחַשׁ דָוִד בְּלִבֵּיהּ בָּתַר כֵּן דִמְנָא יַת עַמָא וַאֲמַר דָוִד קֳדָם יְיָ חָבִית לַחֲדָא דַעֲבָדִית הָדָא וּכְעַן יְיָ אַעֲבַּר כְּעַן יַת חוֹבָא דְעַבְדָךְ אֲרֵי אִתְתַּכָּלִית לַחֲדָא:


But David was troubled in his heart, after he had counted the people. And David said before the LORD, “I am terribly guilty in what I have done. But now, O LORD, take away the guilt of thy servant; for I have acted very foolishly.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:11)

And when David arose in the morning, a prophetic word from before the LORD came to Gad the prophet, David’s seer, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:12)


אֱזִיל וּתְמַלֵיל עִם דָוִד כִּדְנַן אֲמַר יְיָ חֲדָא מִתְּלַת אֲנָא רָמֵי עֲלָךְ בְּחַר לָךְ חַד מִנְהוֹן וְאַעְבֵּד לָךְ:


“Go and speak with David, ‘Thus says the LORD, One of three things I will impose upon you; choose one of them, that I may do it to you.’”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:13)


וַאֲתָא גָד לְוַת דָוִד וְחַוֵי לֵיהּ וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ הֲתָעוֹל לָךְ שְׁבַע שְׁנִין כַּפְנָא בְּאַרְעָךְ אִם תְּלָתָא יַרְחִין תְּהֵי עָרִיק קֳדָם סַנְאָךְ וְהוּא רָדִיף לָךְ וְאִם מֶהֱוֵה תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין מוֹתָא בְּאַרְעָךְ כְּעַן דַע וַחֲזֵי מָה אֲתִיב שֹׁלְחִי פִּתְגָמָא:


So Gad came to David and told him, and said to him, “Shall seven years of famine come to you in your land? Or will you be fleeing three months before your hater, while he is pursuing you? Or shall there be three days of death in your land? Now, consider and decide what I shall answer to Him who sent me.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:14

וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְגָד עָקַת לִי לַחֲדָא נִתְמְסַר כְּעַן בְּיַד מֵימְרָא דַייָ אֲרֵי סַגִיאִין רַחֲמוֹהִי וּבִידָא דֶאֱנָשָׁא לָא אִתְמְסָר:


Then David said to Gad, “I am in great distress; let us be surrendered now into the hand of the speech of the LORD, for his mercy is great. But let me not be surrendered into the hand of man.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:15)


וִיהַב יְיָ מוֹתָא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל מֵעִידַן דְמִתְנְכִיס תְּמִידָא וְעַד דְמִתַּסֵק וּמִית מִן עַמָא מִדָן וְעַד בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע שַׁבְעִין אַלְפִין גַבְרָא:


So the LORD sent death among Israel, from the time that the continual offering is slaughtered until it is sacrificed. And there died from the people, from Dan to Beer sheba, seventy thousand men.

וְאוֹשִׁיט יְדֵיהּ מַלְאָכָא לִירוּשְׁלֵם לְחַבָּלוּתָהּ וְתַב יְיָ מִן בִּישְׁתָא וַאֲמַר לְמַלְאָכָא דִמְחַבֵּל בְּעַמָא מִסַת כְּעַן אַגַח יְדָךְ וּמַלְאָכָא דַייָ הֲוָה שָׁרֵי בְּבֵית אִידְרֵי דְאַרְנוֹן יְבוּסָאָה:


And when the angel stretched forth his hand toward Jerusalem to harm it, the LORD turned back from the evil, and said to the angel who was harming among the people, “Enough now! Stay your hand.” And the angel of the LORD was by the threshing floor of Arvan the Jebusite.

וַאֲמַר דָוִד קֳדָם יְיָ כַּד חֲזָא יַת מַלְאָכָא דְמִקַטֵל בְּעַמָא וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא חָבִית וַאֲנָא סְרָחִית וְאִלֵין עַמָא דְאִינוּן כְּעָנָא בְּיַד רַעְיָא מָה עֲבָדוּ תְּהֵי כְּעַן מָחֲתָךְ בִּי וּבְבֵית אַבָּא:


And David said before the LORD, when he saw the angel who was killing among the people. And he said, “Behold, I have sinned myself, and I have spoilt the whole thing. But these people, who are like sheep in the hand of the shepherd, what have they done? Let thy plague be against me and against my father’s house.”

And Gad came that day to David, and said to him, “Go up, rear an altar before the LORD on the threshing floor of Arvan the Jebusite.

So David went up according to Gad’s word, as the LORD had spoken.

###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:21

And Arvan said, “Why has my lord the king come to his servant?” David said, “To buy the threshing floor of you, in order to build an altar before the LORD, that the epidemic may be averted from the people.”

Then Arvan said to David, “Let my lord the king take and sacrifice what seems right in his sight. See the oxen fit for the burnt offering, and the threshing sledges and the tools of the oxen for the wood.

All that the king has asked him Arvan gives to the king.” And Arvan said to the king, “May the LORD your God accept your sacrifice with pleasure.”


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:24)

But the king said to Arvan, “No, for I will surely buy it from you for a price. I will not bring up burnt offerings before the LORD my God for nothing.” So David bought the threshing floor and the oxen for fifty *selas*.


###### Targum Jonathan on II Samuel 24:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/24:25)


וּבְנָא תַמָן דָוִד מַדְבְּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְאַסֵיק עֲלַוָן וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין וְקַבֵּיל יְיָ צְלוֹת דַיָרֵי אַרְעָא וְאִתְכְּלִי מוֹתָנָא מֵעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And David built there an altar before the LORD, and brought up burnt offerings and holy sacrifices. And the LORD accepted the prayer of the inhabitants of the land, and the epidemic was averted from Israel.
